import type { Metadata } from "next";

  title: "Agent Portal Login",
};

 * Agent portal sign-in. Entry point for the AGENT role, which lands in the
 * /agent namespace.
 *

 * Hence the separate door and the orange brand treatment.
 */

  return (

===== src\app\(portal)\admin\agents\[id]\page.tsx =====
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound, redirect } from "next/navigation";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

import { ApiError } from "@/app/lib/api-error";
import { getCurrentUser, requirePermission } from "@/app/lib/dal";
import { BentoCard } from "@/components/shared/bento-card";
import { DataUnavailable } from "@/components/shared/data-unavailable";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/shared/empty-state";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/shared/status-badge";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { getAgent, getAgentStaff } from "@/lib/api/agent";
import {
  formatDate,
  formatMoney,
  formatNumber,
  initials,
} from "@/lib/format";
import type { AgentResponse, ChargeType, EmployeeSummaryResponse } from "@/types";

/**
 * Agent detail: contract terms and the staff that agent has deployed.
 *
 * Both reads exist today â€” `GET /agents/{id}` and `GET /employees/by-agent/{id}`
 * â€” so the roster and every contract field are live.
 *
 * Gated on `agent:read`, not `agent:write`: the backend opens this record to
 * PAYROLL_OFFICER as well, and a read page that bounces a role the API admits
 * is a bug in the gate, not in the API. The write controls are gated
 * individually instead, because the two writes have *different* audiences â€”
 * `PUT /agents/{id}` accepts ADMIN, `PUT /agents/{id}/deactivate` is
 * SUPER_ADMIN only.
 *
 * Two cards the placeholder carried are gone rather than stubbed: **outstanding
 * advances** (there is no agent-advance read on this endpoint and no
 * `/agent-advances` client module yet) and **invoice history** (no invoice
 * endpoint, and the placeholder linked into `/manager/*`, a namespace that does
 * not exist in this app).
 */

const CHARGE_TYPE_LABEL: Record<ChargeType, string> = {
  PER_EMPLOYEE: "Per Employee",
  PERCENTAGE_OF_GROSS: "Percentage of Gross",
  FIXED_MONTHLY: "Fixed Monthly",
  CUSTOM: "Custom",
};

/** The rate, read off whichever column the charge type actually uses. */
function chargeValue(agent: AgentResponse): string {
  switch (agent.chargeType) {
    case "PERCENTAGE_OF_GROSS":
      return agent.chargePercentage === null
        ? "â€”"
        : `${agent.chargePercentage.toFixed(2)}%`;
    case "PER_EMPLOYEE":
    case "FIXED_MONTHLY":
      return formatMoney(agent.chargeAmount);
    case "CUSTOM":
      return "Per contract";
  }
}

function Field({ label, value }: { label: string; value: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
      <dt className="font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-on-surface-variant">
        {label}
      </dt>
      <dd className="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface">{value}</dd>
    </div>
  );
}

export default async function AgentDetailPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
}) {
  await requirePermission("agent:read");

  const { id } = await params;
  const agentId = Number(id);

  if (!Number.isInteger(agentId) || agentId <= 0) notFound();

  const viewer = await getCurrentUser();

  let agent: AgentResponse;
  let staff: EmployeeSummaryResponse[];

  try {
    [agent, staff] = await Promise.all([
      getAgent(agentId),
      getAgentStaff(agentId),
    ]);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ApiError && error.isUnauthorized) redirect("/login");
    if (error instanceof ApiError && error.isNotFound) notFound();

    return (
      <DataUnavailable
        title="Labour Agent"
        description="Contract terms and deployed staff."
        subject="This agent"
        reason={error instanceof ApiError ? error.message : undefined}
      />
    );
  }

  const canEdit = viewer.role === "SUPER_ADMIN" || viewer.role === "ADMIN";
  const canDeactivate = viewer.role === "SUPER_ADMIN";

  return (
    <>
      {/* â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ page header â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */}
      <div className="mb-8 flex flex-col items-start justify-between gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <Link
            href="/admin/agents"
            className="flex items-center justify-center rounded-full p-1 text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:bg-surface-container-low hover:text-primary"
          >
            <Icon name="chevron_left" />
          </Link>
          <h1 className="font-page-title text-page-title text-on-surface">
            {agent.name}
          </h1>
          <StatusBadge status={agent.status} />
        </div>

        <div className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 sm:w-auto">
          {canEdit ? (
            <button
              type="button"
              className="flex-1 rounded-lg bg-secondary-container px-4 py-2 text-center font-bold text-body-md text-primary-container transition-colors hover:bg-secondary-fixed sm:flex-none"
            >
              Edit Agent
            </button>
          ) : null}
          {/* SUPER_ADMIN only â€” `deactivateAgent` is `hasRole('SUPER_ADMIN')`
              on the backend, so rendering this for ADMIN would be a button
              that always 403s. */}
          {canDeactivate && agent.status === "ACTIVE" ? (
            <button
              type="button"
              className="flex-1 rounded-lg border border-error px-4 py-2 text-center font-bold text-body-md text-error transition-colors hover:bg-error-container sm:flex-none"
            >
              Deactivate
            </button>
          ) : null}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-gutter lg:grid-cols-12">
        {/* â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ main column â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */}
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-gutter lg:col-span-8">
          <BentoCard
            title="Deployed Staff"
            subtitle={`${formatNumber(staff.length)} ${
              staff.length === 1 ? "employee" : "employees"
            } supplied by this agent`}
          >
            {staff.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState
                symbol="group_off"
                title="No staff deployed"
                description="Employees assigned to this agent will be listed here."
              />
            ) : (
              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="w-full border-collapse text-left">
                  <thead>
                    <tr className="border-b border-surface-variant">
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-on-surface-variant">
                        Employee
                      </th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-on-surface-variant">
                        Branch
                      </th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-on-surface-variant">
                        Position
                      </th>
                      <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-on-surface-variant">
                        Status
                      </th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {staff.map((employee) => (
                      <tr
                        key={employee.id}
                        className="border-b border-surface-variant transition-colors last:border-0 hover:bg-background"
                      >
                        <td className="px-4 py-3">
                          <span className="block font-medium text-on-surface">
                            {employee.fullName}
                          </span>
                          <span className="block font-mono text-[10px] text-outline">
                            {employee.employeeNumber}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-on-surface-variant">
                          {employee.branchName ?? "â€”"}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-on-surface-variant">
                          {employee.position ?? "â€”"}
                        </td>
                        <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">
                          <StatusBadge status={employee.employmentStatus} />
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            )}
          </BentoCard>

          <BentoCard
            title="Contract Terms"
            subtitle={
              canEdit
                ? undefined
                : "Read-only â€” contract terms are edited by an administrator."
            }
          >
            {/* Read-only rather than a form: the update endpoint exists, but the
                server action that would persist it lands with the write batch.
                A form whose Save button does nothing is worse than a display. */}
            <dl className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-x-6 gap-y-4 md:grid-cols-2">
              <Field
                label="Charge Type"
                value={CHARGE_TYPE_LABEL[agent.chargeType]}
              />
              <Field label="Rate" value={chargeValue(agent)} />
              <Field
                label="VAT Registered"
                value={agent.vatRegistered ? "Yes" : "No"}
              />
              <Field label="KRA PIN" value={agent.kraPin ?? "â€”"} />
              <Field
                label="Contract Start"
                value={formatDate(agent.contractStartDate)}
              />
              <Field
                label="Contract End"
                value={formatDate(agent.contractEndDate)}
              />
            </dl>
          </BentoCard>
        </div>

        {/* â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ side column â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */}
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-gutter lg:col-span-4">
          <BentoCard title="Contact">
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center">
              <span
                aria-hidden
                className="mb-4 flex size-20 items-center justify-center rounded-full border-4 border-white bg-secondary-container text-xl font-bold text-primary shadow-ambient-1"
              >
                {initials(agent.contactPerson ?? agent.name)}
              </span>
              <h2 className="font-body-md text-body-md font-semibold text-on-surface">
                {agent.contactPerson ?? "No contact person on file"}
              </h2>
            </div>

            <dl className="mt-4 flex flex-col gap-3 font-body-md text-body-md">
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
                <dt className="text-on-surface-variant">Phone</dt>
                <dd className="text-right font-mono text-[11px] text-on-surface">
                  {agent.phone ?? "â€”"}
                </dd>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
                <dt className="text-on-surface-variant">Email</dt>
                <dd className="min-w-0 break-all text-right text-on-surface">
                  {agent.email ?? "â€”"}
                </dd>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
                <dt className="text-on-surface-variant">Address</dt>
                <dd className="text-right text-on-surface">
                  {agent.address ?? "â€”"}
                </dd>
              </div>
            </dl>
          </BentoCard>

          <BentoCard title="Settlement">
            <dl className="flex flex-col gap-3 font-body-md text-body-md">
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
                <dt className="text-on-surface-variant">Bank</dt>
                <dd className="text-right text-on-surface">
                  {agent.bankName ?? "â€”"}
                </dd>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
                <dt className="text-on-surface-variant">Account</dt>
                <dd className="text-right font-mono text-[11px] text-on-surface">
                  {agent.bankAccountNumber ?? "â€”"}
                </dd>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
                <dt className="text-on-surface-variant">Deployed</dt>
                <dd className="text-right font-semibold text-on-surface">
                  {formatNumber(agent.employeeCount ?? staff.length)}
                </dd>
              </div>
            </dl>
          </BentoCard>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}


===== src\app\(portal)\admin\agents\page.tsx =====
import Link from "next/link";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";

import { ApiError } from "@/app/lib/api-error";
import { requirePermission } from "@/app/lib/dal";
import { BentoCard } from "@/components/shared/bento-card";
import { DataUnavailable } from "@/components/shared/data-unavailable";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/shared/empty-state";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/shared/page-header";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/shared/status-badge";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { getAgents } from "@/lib/api/agent";
import { formatMoney, formatNumber, formatPercent, initials } from "@/lib/format";
import type { AgentResponse, ChargeType } from "@/types";

/**
 * Labour agents directory.
 *
 * Every figure on this screen comes from `GET /agents`, which returns the whole
 * table as `List<AgentResponse>` â€” there is no paging, so there are no page
 * controls either.
 *
 * Two tiles the Stitch brief asks for are absent rather than faked:
 * "Pending Commissions" and a month-over-month agent delta. Neither has a
 * source in the schema or the API â€” `agent_payroll_summaries` holds commission
 * only once a payroll run has produced it, and nothing tracks an agent
 * creation trend. An invented number on a financial screen is worse than no
 * number, so the layout drops them and keeps what is real.
 */

const CHARGE_TYPE_LABEL: Record<ChargeType, string> = {
  PER_EMPLOYEE: "Per Employee",
  PERCENTAGE_OF_GROSS: "% of Gross",
  FIXED_MONTHLY: "Fixed Monthly",
  CUSTOM: "Custom",
};

const CHARGE_TYPE_CHIP: Record<ChargeType, string> = {
  PER_EMPLOYEE: "bg-blue-soft text-primary-container",
  PERCENTAGE_OF_GROSS: "bg-tertiary-container/10 text-tertiary-container",
  FIXED_MONTHLY: "bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface-variant",
  CUSTOM: "bg-surface-container-low text-on-surface-variant",
};

/** Renders whichever of the two charge columns actually carries this agent's rate. */
function chargeLabel(agent: AgentResponse): string {
  switch (agent.chargeType) {
    case "PERCENTAGE_OF_GROSS":
      // Two decimals: the column is DECIMAL(5,2) and a commission rate rounded
      // to a whole percent would misstate the contract.
      return agent.chargePercentage === null
        ? "â€”"
        : `${formatPercent(agent.chargePercentage, 2)} of gross`;
    case "PER_EMPLOYEE":
    case "FIXED_MONTHLY":
      return agent.chargeAmount === null
        ? "â€”"
        : formatMoney(agent.chargeAmount);
    case "CUSTOM":
      return "Per contract";
  }
}

export default async function AdminAgentsPage() {
  await requirePermission("agent:read");

  let agents: AgentResponse[];

  try {
    agents = await getAgents();
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ApiError && error.isUnauthorized) redirect("/login");

    return (
      <DataUnavailable
        title="Labour Agents"
        description="Manage external recruitment and labour supply partners."
        subject="The agent directory"
        reason={error instanceof ApiError ? error.message : undefined}
      />
    );
  }

  const activeAgents = agents.filter((agent) => agent.status === "ACTIVE");
  const totalDeployed = agents.reduce(
    (sum, agent) => sum + (agent.employeeCount ?? 0),
    0,
  );

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        title="Labour Agents"
        description="Manage external recruitment and labour supply partners."
        actions={
          <button
            type="button"
            className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-on-primary-fixed-variant px-4 py-2 font-bold text-white shadow-sm transition-colors hover:bg-primary hover:shadow-md"
          >
            <Icon name="add" className="text-sm" />
            Register Agent
          </button>
        }
      />

      <div className="grid grid-cols-12 gap-gutter">
        <BentoCard
          title={null}
          className="col-span-12 flex flex-col justify-between lg:col-span-6"
        >
          <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
            <span className="font-body-md font-medium text-on-surface-variant">
              Active Agents
            </span>
            <span className="flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary-container/10 text-primary">
              <Icon name="storefront" className="text-lg" />
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="mt-4">
            <h2 className="font-card-stat text-card-stat text-on-background">
              {formatNumber(activeAgents.length)}
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-[10px] font-medium text-on-surface-variant">
              of {formatNumber(agents.length)} registered
            </p>
          </div>
        </BentoCard>

        <BentoCard
          title={null}
          className="col-span-12 flex flex-col justify-between lg:col-span-6"
        >
          <div className="flex items-start justify-between">
            <span className="font-body-md font-medium text-on-surface-variant">
              Deployed Staff
            </span>
            <span className="flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-tertiary-container/10 text-tertiary-container">
              <Icon name="group_add" className="text-lg" />
            </span>
          </div>
          <div className="mt-4">
            <h2 className="font-card-stat text-card-stat text-on-background">
              {formatNumber(totalDeployed)}
            </h2>
            <p className="mt-1 text-[10px] font-medium text-on-surface-variant">
              Outsourced employees across all agents
            </p>
          </div>
        </BentoCard>

        <div className="col-span-12 overflow-hidden rounded-card bg-surface-container-lowest shadow-ambient-1">
          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-[#E9EFF5] p-4">
            <h3 className="font-body-md font-bold text-on-background">
              Registered Agents Directory
            </h3>
          </div>

          {agents.length === 0 ? (
            <div className="p-4">
              <EmptyState
                symbol="storefront"
                title="No agents registered"
                description="Labour agents appear here once they have been added to the system."
              />
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="w-full overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="data-table w-full border-collapse text-left">
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface-variant">
                      Agent Details
                    </th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface-variant">
                      Contact Person
                    </th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface-variant">
                      Phone
                    </th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-center font-table-header text-table-header uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface-variant">
                      Deployed Staff
                    </th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface-variant">
                      Commission Type
                    </th>
                    <th className="px-4 py-3 text-right font-table-header text-table-header uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface-variant">
                      Status
                    </th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="font-body-md text-on-background">
                  {agents.map((agent) => (
                    <tr
                      key={agent.id}
                      className="group border-b border-[#E9EFF5] transition-colors last:border-0 hover:bg-[#f6fafe]"
                    >
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
                        <Link
                          href={`/admin/agents/${agent.id}`}
                          className="flex items-center gap-3"
                        >
                          <span
                            aria-hidden
                            className="flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded border border-primary-container/20 bg-primary-container/10 text-xs font-bold text-primary"
                          >
                            {initials(agent.name)}
                          </span>
                          <span>
                            <span className="block font-semibold text-primary">
                              {agent.name}
                            </span>
                            <span className="block text-[10px] text-on-surface-variant">
                              {agent.kraPin ? `KRA: ${agent.kraPin}` : `Agent #${agent.id}`}
                            </span>
                          </span>
                        </Link>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
                        {agent.contactPerson ?? (
                          <span className="text-on-surface-variant">â€”</span>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3 font-mono text-[11px]">
                        {agent.phone ?? (
                          <span className="font-body-md text-on-surface-variant">â€”</span>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3 text-center font-semibold">
                        {formatNumber(agent.employeeCount ?? 0)}
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3">
                        <span
                          className={`inline-flex items-center rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-medium ${CHARGE_TYPE_CHIP[agent.chargeType]}`}
                        >
                          {CHARGE_TYPE_LABEL[agent.chargeType]}
                        </span>
                        <span className="mt-1 block text-[10px] text-on-surface-variant">
                          {chargeLabel(agent)}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="px-4 py-3 text-right">
                        <StatusBadge status={agent.status} />
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          )}

          <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-[#E9EFF5] bg-white p-4 text-xs text-on-surface-variant">
            <span>
              {formatNumber(agents.length)}{" "}
              {agents.length === 1 ? "agent" : "agents"}
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

===== src\app\(portal)\agent\advances\page.tsx =====
import { requirePermission } from "@/app/lib/dal";
import { BentoCard } from "@/components/shared/bento-card";
import { MetricCard } from "@/components/shared/metric-card";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/shared/page-header";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { formatDate, formatMoney, formatMoneyCompact, initials } from "@/lib/format";

/**
 * Agent advance requests.
 *
 * â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
 * DATA IS PLACEHOLDER â€” this maps to `POST /agent-advances` and
 * `GET /agent-advances/my-advances`, but the response shape for a list
 * view (with employee name/department) isn't documented. Swap for
 * `await api.get<Paginated<AgentAdvance>>("/agent-advances/my-advances")`
 * once confirmed â€” note `types.AgentAdvance` doesn't carry a "purpose" or
 * "recovery period" field either, so the DTO may need extending.
 * â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
 */

type AdvanceStatus = "PENDING" | "APPROVED" | "REJECTED";

type AdvanceRow = {
  id: number;
  employeeName: string;
  department: string;
  amount: number;
  requestedDate: string;
  purpose: string;
  recoveryMonths: number;
  status: AdvanceStatus;
};

const ADVANCES: readonly AdvanceRow[] = [
  { id: 1, employeeName: "James Ochieng", department: "Sales Dept", amount: 50_000, requestedDate: "2026-10-24", purpose: "Medical Emergency", recoveryMonths: 3, status: "PENDING" },
  { id: 2, employeeName: "Sarah Mutua", department: "Marketing", amount: 25_000, requestedDate: "2026-10-22", purpose: "School Fees", recoveryMonths: 1, status: "APPROVED" },
  { id: 3, employeeName: "David Kamau", department: "IT Support", amount: 100_000, requestedDate: "2026-10-20", purpose: "Car Repair", recoveryMonths: 6, status: "APPROVED" },
  { id: 4, employeeName: "Alice Nduta", department: "HR Dept", amount: 15_000, requestedDate: "2026-10-18", purpose: "Personal", recoveryMonths: 1, status: "REJECTED" },
  { id: 5, employeeName: "Erick Wanjala", department: "Logistics", amount: 80_000, requestedDate: "2026-10-15", purpose: "Home Renovation", recoveryMonths: 4, status: "PENDING" },
  { id: 6, employeeName: "Catherine Mwangi", department: "Finance", amount: 40_000, requestedDate: "2026-10-10", purpose: "Relocation", recoveryMonths: 2, status: "APPROVED" },
];

const STATUS_CHIP: Record<AdvanceStatus, string> = {
  PENDING: "bg-orange/10 text-orange border border-orange/20",
  APPROVED: "bg-green-main/10 text-green-main border border-green-main/20",
  REJECTED: "bg-error/10 text-error border border-error/20",
};

export default async function AgentAdvancesPage() {
  await requirePermission("agentadvance:read:self");

  const pendingCount = ADVANCES.filter((a) => a.status === "PENDING").length;
  const approvedThisMonth = ADVANCES.filter((a) => a.status === "APPROVED").reduce((sum, a) => sum + a.amount, 0);

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        title="Advance Requests"
        description="Manage and track employee salary advances."
        actions={
          <button type="button" className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-orange px-6 py-2 font-bold text-white shadow-md transition-transform hover:-translate-y-0.5">
            <Icon name="add" className="text-sm" />
            Request Advance
          </button>
        }
      />

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-gutter md:grid-cols-3">
        <MetricCard label="Total Pending" value={`${pendingCount} Requests`} icon="pending_actions" tone="tertiary" hint="+2 since last week" />
        <MetricCard label="Approved This Month" value={formatMoneyCompact(approvedThisMonth)} icon="check_circle" tone="tertiary" hint="+15% vs last month" />
        <MetricCard label="Active Recoveries" value="34 Employees" icon="autorenew" tone="primary" hint={`${formatMoney(120_000)} pending recovery`} />
      </div>

      <BentoCard
        className="mt-gutter"
        title="Recent Requests History"
        action={
          <div className="flex gap-2">
            <button type="button" className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md border border-outline-variant px-3 py-1.5 font-body-md text-body-md font-medium text-primary transition-colors hover:bg-surface-container-low">
              <Icon name="filter_list" className="text-[16px]" />
              Filter
            </button>
            <button type="button" className="flex items-center gap-1 rounded-md border border-outline-variant px-3 py-1.5 font-body-md text-body-md font-medium text-primary transition-colors hover:bg-surface-container-low">
              <Icon name="download" className="text-[16px]" />
              Export
            </button>
          </div>
        }
      >
        <div className="w-full overflow-x-auto">
          <table className="data-table w-full border-collapse text-left">
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th className="pb-3 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-on-surface-variant">Employee</th>
                <th className="pb-3 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-on-surface-variant">Amount (KES)</th>
                <th className="pb-3 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-on-surface-variant">Requested</th>
                <th className="pb-3 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-on-surface-variant">Purpose</th>
                <th className="pb-3 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-on-surface-variant">Recovery</th>
                <th className="pb-3 text-right font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-on-surface-variant">Status</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="font-body-md text-on-surface">
              {ADVANCES.map((advance) => (
                <tr key={advance.id} className="border-b border-surface-variant/50 transition-colors hover:bg-surface-container-low">
                  <td className="py-4">
                    {/* Flex on a wrapper, not the <td> â€” a flex cell drops out
                        of the table layout and desyncs from its header. */}
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <span aria-hidden className="flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary-fixed-dim text-xs font-bold text-primary">
                        {initials(advance.employeeName)}
                      </span>
                      <span className="min-w-0">
                        <span className="block truncate font-medium">{advance.employeeName}</span>
                        <span className="block truncate text-xs text-on-surface-variant">{advance.department}</span>
                      </span>
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="py-4 font-medium tabular-nums">{formatMoney(advance.amount, { showCurrency: false })}</td>
                  <td className="whitespace-nowrap py-4 text-on-surface-variant">{formatDate(advance.requestedDate)}</td>
                  <td className="py-4">{advance.purpose}</td>
                  <td className="whitespace-nowrap py-4">{advance.recoveryMonths} Month{advance.recoveryMonths > 1 ? "s" : ""}</td>
                  <td className="py-4 text-right">
                    <span className={`inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium ${STATUS_CHIP[advance.status]}`}>
                      {advance.status.charAt(0) + advance.status.slice(1).toLowerCase()}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
        <div className="mt-6 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-3 border-t border-surface-variant pt-4 text-sm text-on-surface-variant">
          <span>Showing 1 to {ADVANCES.length} of 24 requests</span>
          <div className="flex gap-1">
            <button type="button" disabled className="rounded px-2 py-1 hover:bg-surface-container-high disabled:opacity-50">
              &lt; Prev
            </button>
            <button type="button" className="rounded bg-primary px-2 py-1 text-white">1</button>
            <button type="button" className="rounded px-2 py-1 hover:bg-surface-container-high">2</button>
            <button type="button" className="rounded px-2 py-1 hover:bg-surface-container-high">3</button>
            <button type="button" className="rounded px-2 py-1 hover:bg-surface-container-high">Next &gt;</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </BentoCard>
    </>
  );
}

                            {initials(inv.staffName)}
                          </span>
                          {inv.staffName}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-4 text-right font-semibold tabular-nums">{formatMoney(inv.amount, { showCurrency: false })}</td>
                      <td className="py-4">
                        <span className={`inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold ${STATUS_CHIP[inv.status]}`}>
                          <span className={`mr-1.5 size-1.5 rounded-full ${STATUS_DOT[inv.status]}`} />
                          {inv.status.charAt(0) + inv.status.slice(1).toLowerCase()}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-4 pr-2 text-center">
                        <button type="button" className="text-on-surface-variant hover:text-primary">
                          <Icon name="more_vert" className="text-[18px]" />
                        </button>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </BentoCard>
        </div>

        {/* â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ side column â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */}
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-gutter lg:col-span-4">
          <BentoCard title="Invoice Status">
            <DonutChart segments={STATUS_SEGMENTS} size={180} legend="value" centerLabel="Total" />
          </BentoCard>

          <div className="rounded-card border border-error/10 bg-gradient-to-br from-white to-[#fff0f0] p-card-padding shadow-ambient-1">
            <div className="mb-4 flex items-center justify-between">
              <h3 className="flex items-center gap-2 font-page-title text-base font-bold text-error">
                <Icon name="warning" className="text-[20px]" />
                Priority Overdue
              </h3>
              <span className="rounded-full bg-error-container px-2 py-1 text-[10px] font-bold text-on-error-container">
                {OVERDUE.length} Invoices
              </span>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
              {OVERDUE.map((item) => (
                <div key={item.id} className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-error/20 bg-white p-3 shadow-sm transition-colors hover:border-error/40">
                  <div>
                    <p className="mb-0.5 text-sm font-semibold text-on-surface">{item.invoiceNo}</p>
                    <p className="text-[10px] text-on-surface-variant">{item.period}</p>
                  </div>
                  <div className="text-right">
                    <p className="text-sm font-bold text-error">{formatMoney(item.amount)}</p>
                    <p className="flex items-center justify-end gap-1 text-[10px] text-error">
                      <Icon name="schedule" className="text-[10px]" />
                      {item.daysLate}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
            <button type="button" className="mt-4 w-full rounded-lg py-2 text-center text-xs font-semibold text-error transition-colors hover:bg-error/5">
              Send Reminders
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

 *
 * â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

 * dedicated payroll audit endpoint if one exists) when ready.

            }
          >
            <div className="w-full overflow-x-auto">
              <table className="data-table w-full border-collapse text-left">
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th className="py-3 pl-2 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface-variant">Run ID</th>
                    <th className="py-3 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface-variant">Actor</th>
                    <th className="py-3 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface-variant">Action</th>
                    <th className="py-3 text-right font-table-header text-table-header uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface-variant">Amount Changed</th>
                    <th className="py-3 pr-2 text-right font-table-header text-table-header uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface-variant">Timestamp</th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody className="font-body-md text-body-md">
                  {ROWS.map((row) => (
                    <tr key={row.id} className="border-b border-surface-variant transition-colors last:border-0 hover:bg-surface-bright">
                      <td className="py-3 pl-2 font-medium text-on-surface">{row.runId}</td>
                      <td className="py-3 text-on-surface-variant">
                        <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          {row.isSystem ? (
                            <Icon name="settings_suggest" className="text-[16px] text-primary" />
                          ) : (
                            <span aria-hidden className="flex size-6 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-secondary-container text-[10px] font-bold text-on-secondary-container">
                              {row.actorInitials}
                            </span>
                          )}
                          {row.actor}
                        </span>
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 text-on-surface-variant">{row.action}</td>
                      <td className="py-3 text-right">
                        {row.amountChanged ? (
                          <span className={`inline-flex items-center rounded px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-medium ${row.significant ? "bg-error-container text-error" : "text-on-surface-variant"}`}>
                            {row.amountChanged}
                          </span>
                        ) : (
                          <span className="font-medium text-on-surface-variant">â€”</span>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td className="py-3 pr-2 text-right text-on-surface-variant">{row.timestamp}</td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
          </BentoCard>
        </div>

        {/* â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ side column â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */}
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-gutter md:col-span-4">
          <SegmentStrip
            segments={[
              { label: "Reviewed", value: formatNumber(0) },
              { label: "Flagged", value: formatNumber(ROWS.filter((r) => r.significant).length), tone: "warning" },
              { label: "Pending", value: formatNumber(0) },
            ]}
          />

          <BentoCard title="Actions by Type">
            <ProgressRows rows={ACTIONS_BY_TYPE} />
          </BentoCard>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

                          Failed
                        </span>
                        {p.failReason && <p className="mt-1 max-w-[150px] truncate text-[10px] text-error">{p.failReason}</p>}
                      </>
                    )}
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-6 py-4 text-right">
                    {p.status === "Succeeded" ? (
                      <button className="text-outline transition-colors hover:text-primary">
                        <Icon name="more_vert" className="text-[20px]" />
                      </button>
                    ) : (
                      <button className="ml-auto flex items-center justify-end gap-1 text-sm font-medium text-primary-container transition-colors hover:text-primary">
                        <Icon name="refresh" className="text-[16px]" />
                        Retry

            <button className="flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded bg-primary-container text-sm font-medium text-white">1</button>

              <Icon name="chevron_right" className="text-[18px]" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

function SummaryTile({
  label,
  icon,
  iconBg,
  iconColor,
  valueClass = "text-on-surface",
  children,
}: {
  label: string;
  icon: string;
  iconBg: string;
  iconColor: string;
  valueClass?: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="group relative overflow-hidden rounded-2xl bg-white p-6 shadow-[0_2px_8px_rgba(11,42,69,.06)] transition-all duration-300 hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:shadow-[0_8px_24px_rgba(11,42,69,.10)]">
      <div className="mb-4 flex items-start justify-between">
        <span className="text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface-variant">{label}</span>
        <div className={`flex size-10 items-center justify-center rounded-full ${iconBg} ${iconColor}`}>
          <Icon name={icon} />
        </div>
      </div>
      <div className={`flex items-baseline gap-1 text-2xl font-bold ${valueClass}`}>{children}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

// src/app/(portal)/finance/disbursements/page.tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { requirePermission } from "@/app/lib/dal";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";

/**
 * â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
 * DATA IS PLACEHOLDER â€” swap for `await api.get<DisbursementBatch[]>("/finance/disbursements")`.
 * â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
 */
interface DisbursementBatch {
  id: string;
  batchId: string;
  payrollRun: string;
  branch: string;
  employees: number;
  totalAmount: string;
  method: "Bank Transfer" | "Mobile Money";
  status: "Completed" | "Processing" | "Failed";
}

const BATCHES: DisbursementBatch[] = [
  { id: "1", batchId: "#DB-2608-001", payrollRun: "Aug 2026", branch: "Nairobi CBD", employees: 142, totalAmount: "12,450,000.00", method: "Bank Transfer", status: "Completed" },
  { id: "2", batchId: "#DB-2608-002", payrollRun: "Aug 2026", branch: "Mombasa Hub", employees: 85, totalAmount: "7,210,500.00", method: "Mobile Money", status: "Processing" },
  { id: "3", batchId: "#DB-2608-003", payrollRun: "Aug 2026", branch: "Kisumu Branch", employees: 45, totalAmount: "3,850,200.00", method: "Bank Transfer", status: "Failed" },
  { id: "4", batchId: "#DB-2607-001", payrollRun: "Jul 2026", branch: "Nairobi CBD", employees: 140, totalAmount: "12,100,000.00", method: "Bank Transfer", status: "Completed" },
  { id: "5", batchId: "#DB-2607-002", payrollRun: "Jul 2026", branch: "Mombasa Hub", employees: 82, totalAmount: "6,950,000.00", method: "Mobile Money", status: "Completed" },
  { id: "6", batchId: "#DB-2607-003", payrollRun: "Jul 2026", branch: "Kisumu Branch", employees: 45, totalAmount: "3,850,200.00", method: "Bank Transfer", status: "Completed" },
];

function statusBadge(status: DisbursementBatch["status"]) {
  switch (status) {
    case "Completed":
      return "bg-tertiary-fixed text-on-tertiary-fixed-variant";
    case "Processing":
      return "bg-primary-fixed text-on-primary-fixed-variant";
    case "Failed":
      return "bg-error-container text-on-error-container";
  }
}

  await requirePermission("payroll:read");

        </div>
        <button className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-[#0B2A45] px-6 py-2.5 text-sm font-bold text-white shadow-sm transition-all hover:bg-primary-container hover:shadow-md">

              <option>Processing</option>

                <th className="px-6 py-4 text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface-variant">Status</th>
                <th className="px-6 py-4 text-right text-[10px] font-semibold uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface-variant">Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="text-sm text-on-background">
              {BATCHES.map((b) => (
                <tr key={b.id} className="group border-b border-[#E9EFF5] transition-colors hover:bg-background">
                  <td className="px-6 py-4 font-medium text-primary">
                    <Link href={`/finance/disbursements/${b.id}`} className="hover:underline">
                      {b.batchId}

                  <td className="px-6 py-4">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-on-surface-variant">
                      <Icon name={b.method === "Bank Transfer" ? "account_balance" : "smartphone"} className="text-[16px]" />
                      {b.method}
                    </div>
                  </td>
                  <td className="px-6 py-4">
                    <span className={`inline-flex items-center rounded-full px-2.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium ${statusBadge(b.status)}`}>

// src/app/(portal)/finance/invoices/[id]/page.tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { requirePermission } from "@/app/lib/dal";

// src/app/(portal)/finance/invoices/page.tsx
import Link from "next/link";
import { requirePermission } from "@/app/lib/dal";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";

/**
 * â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
 * DATA IS PLACEHOLDER â€” swap for `await api.get<Invoice[]>("/finance/invoices")`.
 * â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
 */
interface Invoice {
  id: string;
  agentName: string;
  invoiceNo: string;
  period: string;
  amount: string;
  dueDate: string;
  dueDateUrgent?: boolean;
  status: "Pending" | "Approved" | "Paid";
}

const INVOICES: Invoice[] = [
  { id: "1", agentName: "Safaricom PLC", invoiceNo: "INV-2023-0491", period: "Oct 2023", amount: "450,000.00", dueDate: "Nov 15, 2023", status: "Pending" },
  { id: "2", agentName: "Equity Group Holdings", invoiceNo: "INV-2023-0488", period: "Oct 2023", amount: "1,250,500.00", dueDate: "Nov 12, 2023", status: "Approved" },
  { id: "3", agentName: "KCB Group", invoiceNo: "INV-2023-0475", period: "Sep 2023", amount: "890,200.00", dueDate: "Oct 30, 2023", status: "Paid" },
  { id: "4", agentName: "Jubilee Insurance", invoiceNo: "INV-2023-0495", period: "Oct 2023", amount: "320,000.00", dueDate: "Nov 05, 2023", dueDateUrgent: true, status: "Pending" },
  { id: "5", agentName: "Britam Holdings", invoiceNo: "INV-2023-0498", period: "Oct 2023", amount: "150,750.00", dueDate: "Nov 20, 2023", status: "Pending" },
  { id: "6", agentName: "Nairobi Hospital", invoiceNo: "INV-2023-0460", period: "Sep 2023", amount: "560,000.00", dueDate: "Oct 15, 2023", status: "Paid" },
];

function statusBadge(status: Invoice["status"]) {
  switch (status) {
    case "Pending":
      return "bg-secondary-container text-on-secondary-container border-secondary-container/50";
    case "Approved":
      return "bg-tertiary-fixed text-on-tertiary-fixed border-tertiary-fixed/50";
    case "Paid":
      return "bg-surface-container-high text-on-surface-variant border-surface-variant";
  }
}

===== src\app\(portal)\hr\agents\[id]\page.tsx =====
import Link from "next/link";
import { notFound } from "next/navigation";
import type { ReactNode } from "react";

import { ApiError } from "@/app/lib/api-error";
import { requirePermission } from "@/app/lib/dal";
import { BentoCard } from "@/components/shared/bento-card";
import { DataUnavailable } from "@/components/shared/data-unavailable";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/shared/empty-state";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/shared/page-header";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/shared/status-badge";
import { buttonVariants } from "@/components/ui/button-variants";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { getAgent, getAgentStaff } from "@/lib/api/agent";
import { formatDate, formatMoney, formatNumber, humanizeEnum, initials } from "@/lib/format";
import type { AgentResponse, EmployeeSummaryResponse } from "@/types";

/**
 * One labour agent, read-only.
 *
 * HR can read an agent but not write one â€” `POST`/`PUT /agents` are
 * SUPER_ADMIN/ADMIN and `PUT /agents/{id}/deactivate` is SUPER_ADMIN alone.
 * Contract terms are therefore labels, never inputs.
 *
 * The old placeholder's "Outstanding Advances" card and "Recent Invoices" list
 * are gone. Both were invented: advances and invoices are their own modules with
 * their own endpoints, and neither is exposed per-agent to HR, so there is
 * nothing to total. The side column shows the contact and banking details the
 * agent record actually carries instead.
 */
export default async function HrAgentDetailPage({
  params,
}: {
  params: Promise<{ id: string }>;
}) {
  await requirePermission("agent:read");

  const { id } = await params;
  const agentId = Number(id);

  if (!Number.isInteger(agentId) || agentId <= 0) {
    notFound();
  }

  let agent: AgentResponse;
  let staff: EmployeeSummaryResponse[];

  try {
    // Sequential rather than parallel: a 404 on the agent means the staff call
    // is pointless, and the staff read is the one that can 403 for a role that
    // may still read the agent itself.
    agent = await getAgent(agentId);
    staff = await getAgentStaff(agentId);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ApiError && error.isUnauthorized) {
      throw error;
    }

    if (error instanceof ApiError && error.isNotFound) {
      notFound();
    }

    return (
      <DataUnavailable
        title="Labour Agent"
        description="One external staff supplier."
        subject="This agent"
        reason={error instanceof ApiError ? error.message : undefined}
      />
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        title={
          <span className="flex items-center gap-3">
            {agent.name}
            <StatusBadge status={agent.status} />
          </span>
        }
        description={agent.contactPerson ?? "No contact person on file."}
        actions={
          <Link
            href="/hr/agents"
            className={buttonVariants({ variant: "secondary" })}
          >
            <Icon name="arrow_back" className="text-[18px]" />
            All agents
          </Link>
        }
      />

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-gutter lg:grid-cols-12">
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-gutter lg:col-span-8">
          <BentoCard
            title={
              <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Icon name="groups" className="text-[20px] text-primary" />
                Deployed Staff
              </span>
            }
            action={
              <span className="font-body-md text-[11px] text-outline">
                {formatNumber(staff.length)}{" "}
                {staff.length === 1 ? "employee" : "employees"}
              </span>
            }
          >
            {staff.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState
                symbol="person_off"
                title="No staff deployed"
                description="No employee record currently points at this agent."
              />
            ) : (
              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="w-full text-left font-body-md text-body-md">
                  <thead>
                    <tr className="border-b border-outline-variant/50 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-outline">
                      <th className="pb-3 pr-4">Employee</th>
                      <th className="pb-3 pr-4">Branch</th>
                      <th className="pb-3 pr-4">Position</th>
                      <th className="pb-3">Status</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {staff.map((employee) => (
                      <tr
                        key={employee.id}
                        className="border-b border-outline-variant/25 last:border-0 hover:bg-surface-container-low/40"
                      >
                        <td className="py-3 pr-4">
                          <Link
                            href={`/hr/employees/${employee.id}`}
                            className="group flex items-center gap-3"
                          >
                            <span
                              aria-hidden
                              className="flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary-container/20 text-[10px] font-bold text-primary"
                            >
                              {initials(employee.fullName)}
                            </span>
                            <span>
                              <span className="block font-medium text-on-surface group-hover:text-primary">
                                {employee.fullName}
                              </span>
                              <span className="block text-[10px] text-outline">
                                {employee.employeeNumber}
                              </span>
                            </span>
                          </Link>
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 pr-4 text-on-surface-variant">
                          {employee.branchName ?? "â€”"}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 pr-4 text-on-surface-variant">
                          {employee.position ?? "â€”"}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3">
                          <StatusBadge status={employee.employmentStatus} />
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            )}
          </BentoCard>

          <BentoCard
            title={
              <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Icon name="description" className="text-[20px] text-primary" />
                Contract Terms
              </span>
            }
            action={
              <span className="font-body-md text-[11px] text-outline">
                Read-only
              </span>
            }
          >
            <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 md:grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-3">
              <ReadOnlyField
                label="Charge Type"
                value={humanizeEnum(agent.chargeType)}
              />
              <ReadOnlyField
                label="Charge Amount"
                value={
                  agent.chargeAmount === null
                    ? "Not set"
                    : `${formatMoney(agent.chargeAmount)}${
                        agent.chargeType === "PER_EMPLOYEE" ? " / employee" : ""
                      }`
                }
              />
              <ReadOnlyField
                label="Charge Percentage"
                value={
                  agent.chargePercentage === null
                    ? "Not set"
                    : `${agent.chargePercentage}%`
                }
              />
              <ReadOnlyField
                label="Contract Start"
                value={
                  agent.contractStartDate
                    ? formatDate(agent.contractStartDate)
                    : "Not set"
                }
              />
              <ReadOnlyField
                label="Contract End"
                value={
                  agent.contractEndDate ? formatDate(agent.contractEndDate) : "Open"
                }
              />
              <ReadOnlyField
                label="VAT Registered"
                value={
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <Icon
                      name={agent.vatRegistered ? "check_circle" : "cancel"}
                      className={`text-[16px] ${
                        agent.vatRegistered ? "text-tertiary" : "text-outline"
                      }`}
                    />
                    {agent.vatRegistered ? "Yes" : "No"}
                  </span>
                }
              />
            </div>

            <p className="mt-5 text-[11px] text-outline">
              HR can view these terms but not change them â€” editing an agent is
              an administrator action.
            </p>
          </BentoCard>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-gutter lg:col-span-4">
          <BentoCard
            title={
              <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Icon name="contacts" className="text-[20px] text-primary" />
                Contact
              </span>
            }
          >
            <dl className="flex flex-col gap-3">
              <DetailRow label="Contact Person" value={agent.contactPerson} />
              <DetailRow label="Phone" value={agent.phone} />
              <DetailRow label="Email" value={agent.email} />
              <DetailRow label="Address" value={agent.address} />
            </dl>
          </BentoCard>

          <BentoCard
            title={
              <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Icon name="account_balance" className="text-[20px] text-primary" />
                Banking & Statutory
              </span>
            }
          >
            <dl className="flex flex-col gap-3">
              <DetailRow label="Bank" value={agent.bankName} />
              <DetailRow label="Account Number" value={agent.bankAccountNumber} />
              <DetailRow label="KRA PIN" value={agent.kraPin} />
            </dl>
          </BentoCard>

          <BentoCard
            title={
              <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Icon name="receipt_long" className="text-[20px] text-primary" />
                Invoices & Advances
              </span>
            }
          >
            <p className="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant">
              Not shown here. Agent invoices are produced by a payroll run and
              agent advances are their own module, each with its own status and
              history â€” neither is exposed as a per-agent total to HR.
            </p>
            <p className="mt-3 text-[11px] text-outline">
              Showing a figure here would mean inventing one. The agent&rsquo;s
              own portal and the payroll screens are where those numbers live.
            </p>
          </BentoCard>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

function ReadOnlyField({
  label,
  value,
}: {
  label: string;
  value: ReactNode;
}) {
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-1">
      <span className="font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-outline">
        {label}
      </span>
      <span className="rounded-lg border border-outline-variant/60 bg-surface-container-low px-3 py-2 font-body-md text-body-md font-medium text-on-surface">
        {value}
      </span>
    </div>
  );
}

function DetailRow({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string | null }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-4">
      <dt className="font-body-md text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-outline">
        {label}
      </dt>
      <dd className="text-right font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface">
        {value ?? "â€”"}
      </dd>
    </div>
  );
}


===== src\app\(portal)\hr\agents\page.tsx =====
import Link from "next/link";

import { ApiError } from "@/app/lib/api-error";
import { requirePermission } from "@/app/lib/dal";
import { BentoCard } from "@/components/shared/bento-card";
import { DataUnavailable } from "@/components/shared/data-unavailable";
import { EmptyState } from "@/components/shared/empty-state";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/shared/page-header";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/shared/status-badge";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { getAgents } from "@/lib/api/agent";
import { formatDate, formatMoney, formatNumber, humanizeEnum, initials } from "@/lib/format";
import { buildHref, first } from "@/lib/search-params";
import type { AgentResponse, ChargeType } from "@/types";

/**
 * Labour agents â€” HR's read-only view of the external staff suppliers.
 *
 * Read-only is not a design choice. `roles.ts` grants HR_MANAGER `agent:write`,
 * but no agent write endpoint admits the role: `POST` and `PUT /agents` are
 * SUPER_ADMIN/ADMIN, and `PUT /agents/{id}/deactivate` is SUPER_ADMIN alone. So
 * there is no Add/Edit/Deactivate control on this page â€” rendering one would be
 * a button that 403s.
 *
 * Two columns the old placeholder carried are gone because nothing can produce
 * them: "Outstanding" (agent advances are their own module with their own
 * status, not a per-agent total the API exposes) and "Active Contracts" (there
 * is no contract entity â€” an agent has one contract window, the start and end
 * dates on the row itself).
 */
export default async function LabourAgentsPage({
  searchParams,
}: {
  searchParams: Promise<{ status?: string | string[] }>;
}) {
  await requirePermission("agent:read");

  const params = await searchParams;
  const statusFilter = first(params.status);

  let agents: AgentResponse[];

  try {
    agents = await getAgents();
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ApiError && error.isUnauthorized) {
      throw error;
    }

    return (
      <DataUnavailable
        title="Labour Agents"
        description="Suppliers of outsourced staff."
        subject="The agent list"
        reason={error instanceof ApiError ? error.message : undefined}
      />
    );
  }

  const visible =
    statusFilter === "ACTIVE" || statusFilter === "INACTIVE"
      ? agents.filter((agent) => agent.status === statusFilter)
      : agents;

  const byHeadcount = [...agents]
    .sort((a, b) => (b.employeeCount ?? 0) - (a.employeeCount ?? 0))
    .slice(0, 5);

  const chargeSplit = splitByChargeType(agents);

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        title="Labour Agents"
        description="Suppliers of outsourced staff."
      />

      <div className="mb-4 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
        <FilterChip label="All" count={agents.length} href="/hr/agents" active={!statusFilter} />
        <FilterChip
          label="Active"
          count={agents.filter((agent) => agent.status === "ACTIVE").length}
          href={buildHref("/hr/agents", { status: "ACTIVE" })}
          active={statusFilter === "ACTIVE"}
        />
        <FilterChip
          label="Inactive"
          count={agents.filter((agent) => agent.status === "INACTIVE").length}
          href={buildHref("/hr/agents", { status: "INACTIVE" })}
          active={statusFilter === "INACTIVE"}
        />
      </div>

      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-gutter lg:grid-cols-12">
        <div className="lg:col-span-8">
          <BentoCard
            title={
              <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Icon name="handshake" className="text-[20px] text-primary" />
                Agents
              </span>
            }
            action={
              <span className="font-body-md text-[11px] text-outline">
                {formatNumber(visible.length)}{" "}
                {visible.length === 1 ? "agent" : "agents"}
              </span>
            }
          >
            {visible.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState
                symbol="handshake"
                title={statusFilter ? `No ${statusFilter.toLowerCase()} agents` : "No agents"}
                description="Labour agents are created by an administrator; HR sees them but cannot add one."
              />
            ) : (
              <div className="overflow-x-auto">
                <table className="w-full text-left font-body-md text-body-md">
                  <thead>
                    <tr className="border-b border-outline-variant/50 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-outline">
                      <th className="pb-3 pr-4">Agent</th>
                      <th className="pb-3 pr-4">Contact</th>
                      <th className="pb-3 pr-4 text-center">Deployed</th>
                      <th className="pb-3 pr-4">Charge</th>
                      <th className="pb-3 pr-4">Contract</th>
                      <th className="pb-3">Status</th>
                    </tr>
                  </thead>
                  <tbody>
                    {visible.map((agent) => (
                      <tr
                        key={agent.id}
                        className="border-b border-outline-variant/25 last:border-0 hover:bg-surface-container-low/40"
                      >
                        <td className="py-3 pr-4">
                          <Link
                            href={`/hr/agents/${agent.id}`}
                            className="group flex items-center gap-3"
                          >
                            <span
                              aria-hidden
                              className="flex size-9 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded-lg bg-primary-container/20 text-[11px] font-bold text-primary"
                            >
                              {initials(agent.name)}
                            </span>
                            <span>
                              <span className="block font-semibold text-on-surface group-hover:text-primary">
                                {agent.name}
                              </span>
                              <span className="block text-[11px] text-outline">
                                {agent.kraPin ?? "No KRA PIN on file"}
                              </span>
                            </span>
                          </Link>
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 pr-4 text-on-surface-variant">
                          <span className="block">
                            {agent.contactPerson ?? "â€”"}
                          </span>
                          <span className="block text-[11px] text-outline">
                            {agent.phone ?? agent.email ?? "No contact details"}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 pr-4 text-center tabular-nums text-on-surface-variant">
                          {agent.employeeCount === null
                            ? "â€”"
                            : formatNumber(agent.employeeCount)}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 pr-4 text-on-surface-variant">
                          {describeCharge(agent)}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3 pr-4 text-[12px] text-on-surface-variant">
                          {agent.contractStartDate || agent.contractEndDate
                            ? `${agent.contractStartDate ? formatDate(agent.contractStartDate) : "â€”"} â†’ ${
                                agent.contractEndDate ? formatDate(agent.contractEndDate) : "open"
                              }`
                            : "Not set"}
                        </td>
                        <td className="py-3">
                          <StatusBadge status={agent.status} />
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    ))}
                  </tbody>
                </table>
              </div>
            )}
          </BentoCard>

          <p className="mt-4 text-[11px] text-outline">
            Read-only. HR holds an <code>agent:write</code> permission in the
            frontend role config, but no agent write endpoint accepts the role â€”
            creating, editing and deactivating agents is an administrator task.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-col gap-gutter lg:col-span-4">
          <BentoCard
            title={
              <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Icon name="groups" className="text-[20px] text-primary" />
                Top by Headcount
              </span>
            }
          >
            {byHeadcount.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState
                symbol="groups"
                title="Nothing to rank"
                description="No agents are registered."
              />
            ) : (
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-2">
                {byHeadcount.map((agent) => (
                  <Link
                    key={agent.id}
                    href={`/hr/agents/${agent.id}`}
                    className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg p-2 transition-colors hover:bg-surface-container-low"
                  >
                    <span className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <span
                        aria-hidden
                        className="flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-md bg-primary/10 text-[11px] font-bold text-primary"
                      >
                        {initials(agent.name)}
                      </span>
                      <span className="font-body-md text-body-md font-semibold text-on-surface">
                        {agent.name}
                      </span>
                    </span>
                    <span className="rounded bg-surface-container-low px-2 py-0.5 font-body-md text-body-md font-bold tabular-nums text-on-surface">
                      {agent.employeeCount === null
                        ? "â€”"
                        : formatNumber(agent.employeeCount)}
                    </span>
                  </Link>
                ))}
              </div>
            )}
          </BentoCard>

          <BentoCard
            title={
              <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <Icon name="donut_small" className="text-[20px] text-primary" />
                How Agents Charge
              </span>
            }
          >
            {chargeSplit.length === 0 ? (
              <EmptyState
                symbol="donut_small"
                title="No agents"
                description="The breakdown appears once an agent exists."
              />
            ) : (
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-3">
                {chargeSplit.map((row) => (
                  <div key={row.chargeType} className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between font-body-md text-body-md">
                      <span className="text-on-surface-variant">{row.label}</span>
                      <span className="font-semibold tabular-nums text-on-surface">
                        {formatNumber(row.count)}
                      </span>
                    </div>
                    <div className="h-1.5 overflow-hidden rounded-full bg-surface-container-high">
                      <div
                        className="h-1.5 rounded-full bg-primary-container"
                        style={{ width: `${row.pct}%` }}
                      />
                    </div>
                  </div>
                ))}
                <p className="text-[11px] text-outline">
                  Counted from the {formatNumber(agents.length)}{" "}
                  {agents.length === 1 ? "agent" : "agents"} in the list, not
                  from contracts â€” an agent has one charge type, not a portfolio.
                </p>
              </div>
            )}
          </BentoCard>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

function FilterChip({
  label,
  count,
  href,
  active,
}: {
  label: string;
  count: number;
  href: string;
  active: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <Link
      href={href}
      className={`rounded-full border px-3 py-1.5 font-body-md text-[12px] transition-colors ${
        active
          ? "border-primary-container bg-primary-container/10 font-semibold text-primary"
          : "border-outline-variant text-on-surface-variant hover:bg-surface-container"
      }`}
    >
      {label} ({formatNumber(count)})
    </Link>
  );
}

/**
 * `chargeType` decides which of the two amount columns is meaningful â€” the
 * backend allows both to be set at once, so the type is what makes the number
 * readable rather than the presence of a value.
 */
function describeCharge(agent: AgentResponse): string {
  if (agent.chargeType === "PERCENTAGE_OF_GROSS") {
    return agent.chargePercentage === null
      ? "Percentage â€” rate not set"
      : `${agent.chargePercentage}%`;
  }

  if (agent.chargeAmount === null) {
    return `${humanizeEnum(agent.chargeType)} â€” amount not set`;
  }

  return `${formatMoney(agent.chargeAmount)}${agent.chargeType === "PER_EMPLOYEE" ? "/employee" : ""}`;
}

function splitByChargeType(agents: AgentResponse[]) {
  const counts = new Map<ChargeType, number>();

  for (const agent of agents) {
    counts.set(agent.chargeType, (counts.get(agent.chargeType) ?? 0) + 1);
  }

  return [...counts.entries()]
    .sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1])
    .map(([chargeType, count]) => ({
      chargeType,
      count,
      label: humanizeEnum(chargeType),
      pct: agents.length === 0 ? 0 : Math.round((count / agents.length) * 100),
    }));
}


===== src\app\(portal)\payroll\advances\[id]\page.tsx =====
import { requirePermission } from "@/app/lib/dal";
import { BentoCard } from "@/components/shared/bento-card";
import { PageHeader } from "@/components/shared/page-header";
