import { formatDate, formatMoney } from "@/lib/format";

/**
 * Advance request detail.
 *
 * â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
 * DATA IS PLACEHOLDER â€” no shared `Advance` type yet (see /payroll/advances
 * note). Approve/Reject buttons are not wired to any endpoint yet.
 * â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
 */

const REQUEST = {
  code: "ADV-2026-089",
  employeeName: "David Omondi",
  employeeCode: "ENG-402",
  department: "Engineering",
  dateJoined: "2024-01-15",
  currentNetPay: 120_500,
  amount: 45_000,
  reason: "Medical Emergency - Urgent surgery for family member.",
  recoveryMonth: "September 2026",
  recoveryAmount: 45_000,
  requestedDate: "2026-08-12",
  reviewedDate: "2026-08-13",
};

const TIMELINE: readonly {
  label: string;
  date: string;
  done: boolean;
  current?: boolean;
}[] = [
  { label: "Requested", date: REQUEST.requestedDate, done: true },
  { label: "Reviewed", date: REQUEST.reviewedDate, done: true },
  { label: "Pending Finance", date: "Current Step", done: false, current: true },
];

export default async function AdvanceDetailPage({ params }: { params: Promise<{ id: string }> }) {
  // Gated as `loan:approve` until the backend exposes an employee-advance
  // module â€” see the list page for why. Keeps approve stricter than read.
  await requirePermission("loan:approve");
  const { id } = await params;
  const request = REQUEST; // TODO: fetch by `id` once the endpoint exists

  return (
    <>
      <PageHeader
        title={
          <span className="flex items-center gap-3">
            <button type="button" className="rounded-full p-2 text-on-surface-variant transition-colors hover:bg-surface-container-high">
              <Icon name="arrow_back" />
            </button>
            <span>
              <span className="block">{`Advance Request - ${request.employeeName}`}</span>
              <span className="block font-body-md text-body-md font-medium text-on-surface-variant">
                Request #{request.code}
              </span>
            </span>
          </span>
        }
        actions={
          <span className="rounded-full border border-secondary-container bg-secondary-container px-3 py-1 font-body-md text-body-md font-medium text-on-secondary-container">
            Finance Officer
          </span>
        }
      />

      <div className="grid grid-cols-12 gap-gutter">
        {/* â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ main column â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */}
        <div className="col-span-12 flex flex-col gap-gutter lg:col-span-8">
          <BentoCard title="Approval Workflow">
            <div className="relative flex items-center justify-between">
              <div className="absolute left-0 top-1/2 z-0 h-1 w-full -translate-y-1/2 bg-surface-container-highest" />
              <div className="absolute left-0 top-1/2 z-0 h-1 w-2/3 -translate-y-1/2 bg-primary" />
              {TIMELINE.map((step) => (
                <div key={step.label} className="relative z-10 flex flex-col items-center gap-2">
                  <span
                    className={
                      step.done
                        ? "flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full bg-primary font-bold text-on-primary"
                        : "flex size-8 items-center justify-center rounded-full border-2 border-primary bg-surface-container-lowest font-bold text-primary"
                    }
                  >
                    {step.done ? (
                      <Icon name="check" className="text-sm" />
                    ) : (
                      <Icon name="schedule" className="animate-pulse text-sm" />
                    )}
                  </span>
                  <div className="text-center">
                    <p className={`text-sm font-semibold ${step.current ? "text-primary" : "text-on-surface"}`}>
                      {step.label}
                    </p>
                    <p className="text-xs text-on-surface-variant">
                      {step.done ? formatDate(step.date) : step.date}
                    </p>
                  </div>
                </div>
              ))}
            </div>
          </BentoCard>

          <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-gutter md:grid-cols-2">
            <BentoCard
              title="Request Details"
              action={
                <span className="rounded-lg bg-secondary-container/30 p-2 text-primary">
                  <Icon name="description" />
                </span>
              }
            >
              <div className="mb-4">
                <p className="mb-1 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-on-surface-variant">
                  Reason for Advance
                </p>
                <p className="rounded-lg border border-surface-variant bg-surface-container-low p-3 font-medium leading-relaxed text-on-surface">
                  {request.reason}
                </p>
              </div>
              <div>
                <p className="mb-1 font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-on-surface-variant">
                  Requested Amount
                </p>
                <p className="font-card-stat text-card-stat text-primary">{formatMoney(request.amount)}</p>
              </div>
            </BentoCard>

            <BentoCard
              title="Recovery Plan"
              action={
                <span className="rounded-lg bg-tertiary-container/10 p-2 text-tertiary-container">
                  <Icon name="event_repeat" />
                </span>
              }
              className="border-l-4 border-l-tertiary-container"
            >
              <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-surface-variant bg-surface-container-low p-3">
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <Icon name="calendar_month" className="text-on-surface-variant" />
                    <span>
                      <span className="block text-sm font-semibold text-on-surface">Recovery Month</span>
                      <span className="block text-xs text-on-surface-variant">Scheduled Deduction</span>
                    </span>
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-on-surface">{request.recoveryMonth}</span>
                </div>
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between rounded-lg border border-surface-variant bg-surface-container-low p-3">
                  <span className="flex items-center gap-3">
                    <Icon name="payments" className="text-on-surface-variant" />
                    <span>
                      <span className="block text-sm font-semibold text-on-surface">Recovery Amount</span>
                      <span className="block text-xs text-on-surface-variant">One-time deduction</span>
                    </span>
                  </span>
                  <span className="font-bold text-on-surface">{formatMoney(request.recoveryAmount)}</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </BentoCard>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ side column â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */}
        <div className="col-span-12 flex flex-col gap-gutter lg:col-span-4">
          <BentoCard title={null} className="flex flex-col gap-3">
            <button
              type="button"
              className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-on-primary-fixed-variant px-4 py-3 font-bold text-on-primary transition-colors hover:bg-primary"
            >
              <Icon name="check_circle" />
              Approve Request
            </button>
            <button
              type="button"
              className="flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-error/20 bg-error-container/30 px-4 py-3 font-bold text-error transition-colors hover:bg-error-container"
            >
              <Icon name="cancel" />
              Reject Request
            </button>
          </BentoCard>

          <BentoCard title="Employee Profile">
            <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-4">
              <span
                aria-hidden
                className="flex size-16 shrink-0 items-center justify-center overflow-hidden rounded-full border-2 border-surface-variant bg-secondary-container text-lg font-bold text-primary"
              >
                {request.employeeName.split(" ").map((n) => n[0]).join("")}
              </span>
              <div>
                <h3 className="text-lg font-bold text-on-surface">{request.employeeName}</h3>
                <p className="flex items-center gap-1 text-sm text-on-surface-variant">
                  <Icon name="badge" className="text-[16px]" />
                  {request.employeeCode}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="flex flex-col gap-4">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-outline-variant/30 py-2">
                <span className="text-sm font-medium text-on-surface-variant">Department</span>
                <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-on-surface">{request.department}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-b border-outline-variant/30 py-2">
                <span className="text-sm font-medium text-on-surface-variant">Date Joined</span>
                <span className="text-sm font-semibold text-on-surface">{formatDate(request.dateJoined)}</span>
              </div>
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between py-2">
                <span className="text-sm font-medium text-on-surface-variant">Current Net Pay</span>
                <span className="font-bold text-primary">{formatMoney(request.currentNetPay)}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
          </BentoCard>

          <div className="flex items-start gap-3 rounded-card border border-secondary-container/40 bg-secondary-container/20 p-4">
            <Icon name="info" className="mt-0.5 text-secondary" />
            <div>
              <h4 className="mb-1 text-sm font-bold text-secondary">Advance Policy Reminder</h4>
              <p className="text-xs leading-relaxed text-on-surface-variant">
                Employees are eligible for a salary advance up to 50% of their net pay. The requested amount of{" "}
                {formatMoney(request.amount)} is within the permissible limit for this employee.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

import Link from "next/link";

import { requirePermission } from "@/app/lib/dal";

/**
 * Salary advance requests.
 *
 * â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
 * DATA IS PLACEHOLDER â€” no shared `Advance` type exists in types/index.ts
 * yet (only `AgentAdvance`, which is for labour agents, not employees).
 * `AdvanceRow` below is a local shape until the backend exposes an
 * employee-advance DTO; swap for `await api.get<Paginated<Advance>>(...)`
 * once that endpoint and type exist.
 * â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
 */

type AdvanceStatus = "PENDING" | "APPROVED" | "REJECTED";

type AdvanceRow = {
  id: number;
  employeeName: string;
  employeeCode: string;
  department: string;
  amount: number;

  reason: string;
  recoveryMonth: string;

};

  PENDING: "bg-amber-main/10 text-amber-main border border-amber-main/30",

  REJECTED: "bg-error/10 text-error border border-error/30",

const STATUS_LABEL: Record<AdvanceStatus, string> = {

/**

 *
 * â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

                <th className="px-6 py-4 text-right font-table-header text-table-header uppercase text-on-surface-variant">Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody className="divide-y divide-surface-variant font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface">
              {DOCUMENTS.map((doc) => {
                const meta = TYPE_META[doc.type];
                return (
                  <tr key={doc.id} className="group transition-colors hover:bg-primary/5">
                    <td className="px-6 py-4">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <span className={`flex size-8 shrink-0 items-center justify-center rounded ${meta.iconClass}`}>
                          <Icon name={meta.icon} className="text-sm" />
                        </span>
                        <span className="font-medium">{doc.name}</span>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-6 py-4">
                      <span className={`inline-flex items-center rounded-md px-2 py-1 text-[10px] font-semibold ${meta.chipClass}`}>
                        {meta.label}
                      </span>
                    </td>
                    <td className="px-6 py-4 text-on-surface-variant">{doc.period}</td>
                    <td className="px-6 py-4 text-on-surface-variant">{doc.generated}</td>
                    <td className="px-6 py-4 text-on-surface-variant">{doc.size}</td>
                    <td className="px-6 py-4 text-right">
                      <div className="flex justify-end gap-2 opacity-0 transition-opacity group-hover:opacity-100">

                        >
                          View
                        </button>
                        <button

                          title="Download"
                          className="rounded bg-primary/10 p-1.5 text-primary transition-colors hover:bg-primary/20"
                        >
                          <Icon name="download" className="text-sm" />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </div>
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between border-t border-outline-variant/10 px-6 py-4">
          <span className="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant">Showing 1 to 8 of 45 documents</span>
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <button type="button" className="rounded p-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:bg-surface-variant/50 disabled:opacity-50">
              <Icon name="chevron_left" className="text-sm" />
            </button>
            <button type="button" className="flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded bg-primary text-xs font-medium text-on-primary">
              1
            </button>
            <button type="button" className="flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded text-xs font-medium text-on-surface-variant hover:bg-surface-variant/50">
              2
            </button>
            <button type="button" className="flex size-7 items-center justify-center rounded text-xs font-medium text-on-surface-variant hover:bg-surface-variant/50">
              3
            </button>
            <button type="button" className="rounded p-1 text-on-surface-variant hover:bg-surface-variant/50">
              <Icon name="chevron_right" className="text-sm" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </>
  );
}

                    {formatMoney(row.net, { showCurrency: false })}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
            <tfoot className="bg-surface-bright">
              <tr className="border-t-2 border-surface-container-highest">
                <td className="sticky left-0 z-10 bg-surface-bright p-4 font-body-md text-body-md font-bold uppercase tracking-wide text-on-surface">
                  Totals ({PREVIEW_ROWS.length} Employees)
                </td>

                <td className="p-4 text-right font-body-md text-body-md font-bold tabular-nums text-on-surface-variant">{formatMoney(TOTALS.shif, { showCurrency: false })}</td>
                <td className="p-4 text-right font-body-md text-body-md font-bold tabular-nums text-on-surface-variant">{formatMoney(TOTALS.housingLevy, { showCurrency: false })}</td>
                <td className="p-4 text-right font-body-md text-body-md font-bold tabular-nums text-on-surface-variant">{formatMoney(TOTALS.loanDeduction, { showCurrency: false })}</td>
                <td className="bg-primary/10 p-4 text-right font-card-stat text-card-stat text-primary">{formatMoney(TOTALS.net, { showCurrency: false })}</td>
              </tr>
            </tfoot>
          </table>
        </div>

        <div className="z-20 flex items-center justify-between border-t border-surface-container-highest bg-surface p-card-padding">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-on-surface-variant">
            <Icon name="verified" className="text-[16px] text-tertiary-fixed-dim" />

          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

===== src\app\actions\auth.ts =====
"use server";

import { redirect } from "next/navigation";

import { env } from "@/config/env";
import { ROLE_HOME, isRole, type Role } from "@/config/roles";
import {
  agentLoginSchema,
  changePasswordSchema,
  employeeLoginSchema,
  loginSchema,
} from "@/app/lib/definitions";
import { createSession, deleteSession, getSession } from "@/app/lib/session";
import { toActionError } from "@/app/lib/api-error";
import type { ActionState } from "@/types";

/**
 * Auth actions. Mirrors `controller/auth` + `service/auth`.
 *
 * Every action here is reachable as a direct POST regardless of which
 * component imports it, so each one validates its own input independently.
 */

/**
 * `dto/response/auth/AuthResponse`, unwrapped from its ApiResponse envelope.
 * Identical across all three login surfaces.
 */
type LoginResponse = {
  accessToken: string;
  refreshToken: string;
  userId: number;
  username: string;
  role: string;
  displayName: string;
  employeeId: number | null;
  agentId: number | null;
  branchId: number | null;
  mustChangePassword: boolean;
};

const API_ROOT = env.API_BASE_URL.replace(/\/$/, "");
const AUTH_BASE = `${API_ROOT}/auth`;

/** Deliberately uniform â€” never reveal whether an identifier exists. */
const INVALID_CREDENTIALS = "Incorrect credentials. Please try again.";

async function authenticate(
  endpoint: string,
  payload: Record<string, unknown>,
): Promise<LoginResponse | { error: string }> {
  try {
    const response = await fetch(`${AUTH_BASE}${endpoint}`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: {
        "Content-Type": "application/json",
        Accept: "application/json",
      },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
      cache: "no-store",
    });

    if (!response.ok) {
      if (response.status === 401 || response.status === 403) {
        return { error: INVALID_CREDENTIALS };
      }
      if (response.status === 423 || response.status === 429) {
        return {
          error: "Too many attempts. Your account is locked â€” contact HR.",
        };
      }
      return { error: "Unable to sign in right now. Please try again." };
    }

    const envelope = (await response.json()) as {
      success?: boolean;
      message?: string;
      data?: LoginResponse | null;
    };

    if (!envelope.success || !envelope.data?.accessToken) {
      return { error: INVALID_CREDENTIALS };
    }

    return envelope.data;
  } catch {
    return { error: "Unable to reach the server. Check your connection." };
  }
}

/** Persists the session, then redirects. Never returns. */
async function establishSession(
  result: LoginResponse,
  next?: string,
): Promise<never> {
  await createSession({
    userId: result.userId,
    username: result.username,
    displayName: result.displayName ?? result.username,
    role: result.role as Role,
    employeeId: result.employeeId,
    agentId: result.agentId,
    branchId: result.branchId,
    mustChangePassword: result.mustChangePassword,
    accessToken: result.accessToken,
    refreshToken: result.refreshToken,
  });

  if (result.mustChangePassword) {
    redirect("/set-password");
  }

  // Only same-origin relative paths â€” blocks open redirect via ?next=
  const destination =
    next && next.startsWith("/") && !next.startsWith("//")
      ? next
      : ROLE_HOME[result.role as Role];

  redirect(destination);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ staff login */

export async function login(
  _prev: ActionState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<ActionState> {
  const parsed = loginSchema.safeParse({
    username: formData.get("username"),
    password: formData.get("password"),
    next: formData.get("next") ?? undefined,
  });

  if (!parsed.success) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Check the fields below.",
      fieldErrors: parsed.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  const { username, password, next } = parsed.data;
  const result = await authenticate("/login", { username, password });

  if ("error" in result) {
    return { status: "error", message: result.error };
  }

  if (!isRole(result.role)) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Your account has an unrecognised role. Contact an administrator.",
    };
  }

  return establishSession(result, next);
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------- employee login */

export async function employeeLogin(
  _prev: ActionState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<ActionState> {
  const parsed = employeeLoginSchema.safeParse({
    employeeNumber: formData.get("employeeNumber"),
    pin: formData.get("pin"),
  });

  if (!parsed.success) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Check the fields below.",
      fieldErrors: parsed.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  const result = await authenticate("/employee-login", parsed.data);

  if ("error" in result) {
    return { status: "error", message: result.error };
  }

  if (!isRole(result.role)) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Your account has an unrecognised role. Contact HR.",
    };
  }

  return establishSession(result);
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ agent login */

export async function agentLogin(
  _prev: ActionState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<ActionState> {
  const parsed = agentLoginSchema.safeParse({
    agentCode: formData.get("username"),
    password: formData.get("password"),
  });

  if (!parsed.success) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Check the fields below.",
      fieldErrors: parsed.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  const result = await authenticate("/agent-login", parsed.data);

  if ("error" in result) {
    return { status: "error", message: result.error };
  }

  if (!isRole(result.role)) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Your account has an unrecognised role. Contact support.",
    };
  }

  return establishSession(result);
}

/* --------------------------------------------------- forced password reset */

export async function setPassword(
  _prev: ActionState,
  formData: FormData,
): Promise<ActionState> {
  const session = await getSession();

  if (!session) {
    redirect("/login");
  }

  const parsed = changePasswordSchema.safeParse({
    currentPassword: formData.get("currentPassword"),
    newPassword: formData.get("newPassword"),
    confirmPassword: formData.get("confirmPassword"),
  });

  if (!parsed.success) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: "Check the fields below.",
      fieldErrors: parsed.error.flatten().fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  try {
    // The change-password endpoint lives on UserController, not the auth
    // controller, and is keyed by user id â€” `#userId == authentication
    // .principal.id or hasRole('SUPER_ADMIN')` guards it server-side.
    const response = await fetch(
      `${API_ROOT}/users/${session.userId}/change-password`,
      {
        method: "PUT",
        headers: {
          "Content-Type": "application/json",
          Accept: "application/json",
          Authorization: `Bearer ${session.accessToken}`,
        },
        body: JSON.stringify({
          currentPassword: parsed.data.currentPassword,
          newPassword: parsed.data.newPassword,
        }),
        cache: "no-store",
      },
    );

    if (!response.ok) {
      return {
        status: "error",
        message:
          response.status === 400 || response.status === 401
            ? "Your current password is incorrect."
            : "Unable to update your password. Please try again.",
        fieldErrors:
          response.status === 400 || response.status === 401
            ? { currentPassword: ["Incorrect password."] }
            : undefined,
      };
    }
  } catch (error) {
    return toActionError(error);
  }

  // Clear must_change_password by re-issuing the cookie.
  await createSession({ ...session, mustChangePassword: false });

  redirect(ROLE_HOME[session.role]);
}

/* ----------------------------------------------------------------- logout */

export async function logout(): Promise<void> {
  await deleteSession();
  redirect("/login");
}


===== src\app\api\reports\[kind]\route.ts =====
import type { NextRequest } from "next/server";

import { ApiError } from "@/app/lib/api-error";
import { getSession } from "@/app/lib/session";
import { hasPermission } from "@/config/roles";
import {
  getAttendanceReport,
  getEmployeeListReport,
  getPayrollRegisterReport,
} from "@/lib/api/report";

/**
 * Serves the generated PDFs from `controller/report/ReportController`.
 *
 * â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
 * WHY THIS EXISTS
 *
 * `lib/api/report` is `server-only` and returns a `Blob`, which a Server
 * Component cannot hand to the browser and must not push through the RSC
 * payload. The reports page therefore submits a plain
 * `<form method="get" target="_blank">` straight at this route, which does the
 * authenticated hop server-side and streams the file back. No client
 * JavaScript is involved on either side of it.
 *
 * The backend already sets `Content-Disposition: attachment`. This route
 * deliberately overrides that to `inline`, so clicking a report opens it in the
 * browser's PDF viewer â€” where downloading is one click â€” instead of saving a
 * file into a downloads folder the user then has to find. `?download=1` puts
 * the original behaviour back for a real "Download" button.
 *
 * Errors are answered as plain text, not JSON: the caller is a browser tab
 * showing a document, and a stack of JSON there reads as a broken page. Any
 * status other than 200 leaves the failure legible.
 *
 * `src/proxy.ts` excludes `/api` from its matcher, so this handler is the only
 * gate â€” `requirePermission` would redirect, which is right for a page and
 * wrong here, so the check is repeated without it. Same reasoning as
 * `/api/attendance/live`.
 * â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
 */

/** The reports the backend serves, by the name that appears in the URL. */
const KINDS = ["attendance", "payroll-register", "employee-list"] as const;

type ReportKind = (typeof KINDS)[number];

/**
 * Narrows a path segment to a known report.
 *
 * The tuple has to be widened to `readonly string[]` to call `.includes` on a
 * plain `string` â€” the same shape `isRole` uses in `config/roles.ts`.
 */
function isReportKind(value: string): value is ReportKind {
  return (KINDS as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}

function fail(message: string, status: number): Response {
  return new Response(message, {
    status,
    headers: { "Content-Type": "text/plain; charset=utf-8", "Cache-Control": "no-store" },
  });
}

/** A positive integer id, or `undefined`. A bad id must not become `NaN`. */
function parseId(raw: string | null): number | undefined {
  if (!raw) return undefined;
  const value = Number(raw);
  return Number.isInteger(value) && value > 0 ? value : undefined;
}

/** `yyyy-MM-dd` only â€” the controller's `LocalDate` binding rejects anything else. */
function parseDate(raw: string | null): string | undefined {
  return raw && /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(raw) ? raw : undefined;
}

/** Repeated ids, deduplicated, with anything malformed dropped. */
function parseIds(values: string[]): number[] {
  const ids = values
    .map((value) => Number(value))
    .filter((value) => Number.isInteger(value) && value > 0);

  return [...new Set(ids)];
}

/**
 * Quote-safe filename for the header. The backend chooses it, but it reaches us
 * as an unvalidated string and a `"` in it would truncate the header.
 */
function safeFilename(name: string): string {
  const cleaned = name.replace(/["\\\r\n]/g, "").trim();
  return cleaned || "report.pdf";
}

export async function GET(
  request: NextRequest,
  { params }: { params: Promise<{ kind: string }> },
) {
  const session = await getSession();

  if (!session) {
    return fail("Your session has expired. Please sign in again.", 401);
  }

  if (!hasPermission(session.role, "report:read")) {
    return fail("You do not have permission to generate reports.", 403);
  }

  const { kind } = await params;

  if (!isReportKind(kind)) {
    return fail(
      `Unknown report "${kind}". Expected one of: ${KINDS.join(", ")}.`,
      404,
    );
  }

  const search = request.nextUrl.searchParams;

  let file: { blob: Blob; filename: string };

  try {
    switch (kind) {
      case "attendance": {
        const branchId = parseId(search.get("branchId"));
        const start = parseDate(search.get("start"));
        const end = parseDate(search.get("end"));

        if (!branchId || !start || !end) {
          return fail(
            "An attendance report needs `branchId`, `start` and `end` â€” the date bounds are required, not defaulted.",
            400,
          );
        }

        file = await getAttendanceReport(branchId, { start, end });
        break;
      }

      case "payroll-register": {
        const runId = parseId(search.get("runId"));

        if (!runId) {
          return fail("A payroll register needs a `runId`.", 400);
        }

        file = await getPayrollRegisterReport(runId);
        break;
      }

      case "employee-list": {
        const branchIds = parseIds(search.getAll("branchIds"));

        if (branchIds.length === 0) {
          return fail("An employee list needs at least one `branchIds`.", 400);
        }

        file = await getEmployeeListReport(branchIds);
        break;
      }
    }
  } catch (error) {
    // A 403 here is real, not a stale token: HR is in `REPORT_ROLES` on all
    // three, so a refusal means the parameters named a resource the caller
    // cannot see â€” a branch manager asking for another branch, most often.
    const status = error instanceof ApiError ? error.status : 502;
    const message =
      error instanceof ApiError
        ? error.message
        : "Unable to reach the reporting service.";

    return fail(message, status);
  }

  return new Response(file.blob, {
    headers: {
      "Content-Type": "application/pdf",
      "Content-Disposition": `${search.get("download") === "1" ? "attachment" : "inline"}; filename="${safeFilename(file.filename)}"`,
      "Cache-Control": "no-store",
    },
  });
}


===== src\app\lib\api-client.ts =====
import "server-only";

import { env } from "@/config/env";
import type { Paginated } from "@/types";

import { ApiError, toApiError } from "./api-error";
import { getSession, refreshSession } from "./session";

/**
 * Every backend controller returns `dto/response/ApiResponse` â€” the payload is
 * always one level down under `data`. Unwrapped here so no call site has to
 * know the envelope exists.
 */
type Envelope<T> = {
  success: boolean;
  message: string | null;
  data: T;
  timestamp?: string;
};

/**
 * Server-side fetch wrapper for the Spring Boot API.
 * Mirrors the `controller/` surface; one function per HTTP verb.
 *
 * Never import this from a Client Component â€” it reads the session cookie and
 * carries the backend JWT. Client data flows through Server Components or
 * Server Actions instead.
 */

type QueryValue = string | number | boolean | null | undefined;

export type ApiRequestOptions = {
  query?: Record<string, QueryValue | QueryValue[]>;
  /** Next.js cache tags, for `revalidateTag` after a mutation. */
  tags?: string[];
  /** Seconds. Omit for no-store (default on authenticated reads). */
  revalidate?: number | false;
  signal?: AbortSignal;
  /** Skip the Authorization header (login, password reset). */
  anonymous?: boolean;
};

function buildUrl(
  path: string,
  query?: ApiRequestOptions["query"],
): string {
  const base = env.API_BASE_URL.replace(/\/$/, "");
  const url = new URL(`${base}${path.startsWith("/") ? path : `/${path}`}`);

  if (query) {
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(query)) {
      if (value === undefined || value === null || value === "") continue;

      if (Array.isArray(value)) {
        for (const entry of value) {
          if (entry !== undefined && entry !== null && entry !== "") {
            url.searchParams.append(key, String(entry));
          }
        }
      } else {
        url.searchParams.set(key, String(value));
      }
    }
  }

  return url.toString();
}

async function request<T>(
  method: "GET" | "POST" | "PUT" | "PATCH" | "DELETE",
  path: string,
  body?: unknown,
  options: ApiRequestOptions = {},
): Promise<T> {
  const headers = new Headers({ Accept: "application/json" });
  const isFormData = body instanceof FormData;

  if (body !== undefined && !isFormData) {
    headers.set("Content-Type", "application/json");
  }

  if (!options.anonymous) {
    const session = await getSession();

    if (!session) {
      throw new ApiError(401, "Your session has expired. Please sign in again.");
    }

    headers.set("Authorization", `Bearer ${session.accessToken}`);
  }

  const url = buildUrl(path, options.query);
  const init: RequestInit = {
    method,
    headers,
    body: isFormData ? body : body !== undefined ? JSON.stringify(body) : undefined,
    signal: options.signal,
    // Authenticated reads are per-user; caching them across users would leak.
    cache: options.revalidate === undefined ? "no-store" : undefined,
    next:
      options.revalidate === undefined
        ? undefined
        : { revalidate: options.revalidate, tags: options.tags },
  };

  let response = await send(url, init);

  // The backend access token lasts 15 minutes against an 8-hour cookie, so a
  // 401 mid-session usually means "expired", not "signed out". Refresh once
  // and replay; a second 401 is genuine and falls through to the caller.
  if (response.status === 401 && !options.anonymous) {
    const session = await getSession();
    const accessToken = session ? await refreshSession(session) : undefined;

    if (accessToken) {
      headers.set("Authorization", `Bearer ${accessToken}`);
      response = await send(url, init);
    }
  }

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw await toApiError(response);
  }

  if (response.status === 204) {
    return undefined as T;
  }

  const contentType = response.headers.get("content-type") ?? "";

  if (!contentType.includes("application/json")) {
    return (await response.text()) as T;
  }

  const payload = (await response.json()) as Envelope<T> | T;

  if (!isEnvelope<T>(payload)) {
    // Not every response is wrapped â€” Spring Security rejections and any
    // future raw endpoint come through bare. Pass them straight back.
    return payload;
  }

  // A handful of controllers signal refusal with `ApiResponse.error(...)` on a
  // 200 (LoanController, BiometricSyncController), so an ok status alone does
  // not mean the call succeeded.
  if (!payload.success) {
    throw new ApiError(
      response.status === 200 ? 400 : response.status,
      payload.message?.trim() || "The request was rejected.",
    );
  }

  return payload.data;
}

function isEnvelope<T>(payload: unknown): payload is Envelope<T> {
  return (
    typeof payload === "object" &&
    payload !== null &&
    "success" in payload &&
    typeof (payload as { success: unknown }).success === "boolean" &&
    "data" in payload
  );
}

/** One network hop. Translates transport failures into a 503 ApiError. */
async function send(url: string, init: RequestInit): Promise<Response> {
  try {
    return await fetch(url, init);
  } catch (cause) {
    if (cause instanceof Error && cause.name === "AbortError") throw cause;
    throw new ApiError(503, "Unable to reach the server.");
  }
}

export const api = {
  get: <T>(path: string, options?: ApiRequestOptions) =>
    request<T>("GET", path, undefined, options),

  /** Convenience wrapper for endpoints returning Spring `Page<T>`. */
  getPage: <T>(path: string, options?: ApiRequestOptions) =>
    request<Paginated<T>>("GET", path, undefined, options),

  post: <T>(path: string, body?: unknown, options?: ApiRequestOptions) =>
    request<T>("POST", path, body, options),

  put: <T>(path: string, body?: unknown, options?: ApiRequestOptions) =>
    request<T>("PUT", path, body, options),

  patch: <T>(path: string, body?: unknown, options?: ApiRequestOptions) =>
    request<T>("PATCH", path, body, options),

  delete: <T>(path: string, options?: ApiRequestOptions) =>
    request<T>("DELETE", path, undefined, options),
};

/** Streams a PDF from `service/payroll/documents` for download. */
export async function fetchDocument(
  path: string,
  options: ApiRequestOptions = {},
): Promise<{ blob: Blob; filename: string }> {
  const session = await getSession();

  if (!session) {
    throw new ApiError(401, "Your session has expired. Please sign in again.");
  }

  const response = await fetch(buildUrl(path, options.query), {
    headers: {
      Authorization: `Bearer ${session.accessToken}`,
      Accept: "application/pdf",
    },
    cache: "no-store",
  });

  if (!response.ok) {
    throw await toApiError(response);
  }

  const disposition = response.headers.get("content-disposition") ?? "";
  const match = /filename="?([^"]+)"?/.exec(disposition);

  return {
    blob: await response.blob(),
    filename: match?.[1] ?? "document.pdf",
  };
}


===== src\app\lib\api-error.ts =====
import "server-only";

/**
 * Error envelope parsing. Mirrors `exception/GlobalExceptionHandler`.
 *
 * Parsed in one place so no call site has to guess the response shape.
 */

/** Shape emitted by the Spring GlobalExceptionHandler (`ApiResponse.error`). */
export type ApiErrorBody = {
  success?: boolean;
  message?: string;
  timestamp?: string;
  /**
   * `@Valid` failures land here â€” the handler puts the fieldâ†’message map in
   * the envelope's `data` slot rather than a dedicated `errors` key.
   */
  data?: Record<string, string | string[]> | null;
  /** Spring Boot's own error page shape, for anything short of a controller. */
  status?: number;
  error?: string;
  path?: string;
  errors?: Record<string, string | string[]>;
};

export class ApiError extends Error {
  readonly status: number;
  readonly fieldErrors?: Record<string, string[]>;
  readonly path?: string;

  constructor(
    status: number,
    message: string,
    fieldErrors?: Record<string, string[]>,
    path?: string,
  ) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ApiError";
    this.status = status;
    this.fieldErrors = fieldErrors;
    this.path = path;
  }

  get isUnauthorized(): boolean {
    return this.status === 401;
  }

  get isForbidden(): boolean {
    return this.status === 403;
  }

  get isNotFound(): boolean {
    return this.status === 404;
  }

  get isValidation(): boolean {
    return this.status === 400 || this.status === 422;
  }

  /** 409 â€” optimistic-lock or duplicate-key collision. */
  get isConflict(): boolean {
    return this.status === 409;
  }

  get isServer(): boolean {
    return this.status >= 500;
  }
}

function normalizeFieldErrors(
  errors: Record<string, string | string[]> | null | undefined,
): Record<string, string[]> | undefined {
  if (!errors) return undefined;

  const entries = Object.entries(errors).filter(
    ([, value]) =>
      typeof value === "string" ||
      (Array.isArray(value) && value.every((v) => typeof v === "string")),
  );

  if (entries.length === 0) return undefined;

  return Object.fromEntries(
    entries.map(([field, value]) => [
      field,
      Array.isArray(value) ? value : [value],
    ]),
  );
}

const FALLBACK_MESSAGES: Record<number, string> = {
  400: "The request was invalid.",
  401: "Your session has expired. Please sign in again.",
  403: "You do not have permission to perform this action.",
  404: "The requested record was not found.",
  409: "This record was changed by someone else. Reload and try again.",
  422: "Some fields need attention.",
  500: "Something went wrong on the server.",
  503: "The service is temporarily unavailable.",
};

/** Builds an ApiError from a failed Response. Never throws. */
export async function toApiError(response: Response): Promise<ApiError> {
  let body: ApiErrorBody | undefined;

  try {
    body = (await response.json()) as ApiErrorBody;
  } catch {
    // Empty or non-JSON body (gateway timeout, HTML error page).
  }

  const message =
    body?.message?.trim() ||
    body?.error?.trim() ||
    FALLBACK_MESSAGES[response.status] ||
    `Request failed with status ${response.status}.`;

  return new ApiError(
    response.status,
    message,
    normalizeFieldErrors(asFieldMap(body?.data) ?? body?.errors),
    body?.path,
  );
}

/**
 * `data` is the envelope's generic payload slot, so on a validation failure it
 * holds the fieldâ†’message map but on any other error it is null â€” or, for a
 * hand-rolled error response, could be anything at all. Only a plain object
 * survives; a string would otherwise index into per-character "fields".
 */
function asFieldMap(
  data: unknown,
): Record<string, string | string[]> | undefined {
  if (typeof data !== "object" || data === null || Array.isArray(data)) {
    return undefined;
  }

  return data as Record<string, string | string[]>;
}

/**
 * Converts any thrown value into an `ActionState` error for `useActionState`.
 * Keeps raw internals out of the UI.
 */
export function toActionError(error: unknown): {
  status: "error";
  message: string;
  fieldErrors?: Record<string, string[]>;
} {
  if (error instanceof ApiError) {
    return {
      status: "error",
      message: error.message,
      fieldErrors: error.fieldErrors,
    };
  }

  return {
    status: "error",
    message: "Unable to reach the server. Check your connection and try again.",
  };
}


===== src\app\lib\dal.ts =====
import "server-only";

import { cache } from "react";
import { redirect } from "next/navigation";

import {
  ROLE_HOME,
  canAccessPath,
  hasAnyPermission,
  hasPermission,
  type Permission,
  type Role,
} from "@/config/roles";
import type { CurrentUser } from "@/types";

import { getSession, type SessionPayload } from "./session";

/**
 * Data Access Layer. Mirrors `security/AccessGuard`.
 *
 * Every protected page and Server Action calls through here. Wrapped in
 * React `cache()` so repeated calls inside one render pass hit once.
 *
 * This is a UX gate, not the security boundary â€” the Spring AccessGuard
 * re-authorizes every request regardless of what happens here.
 */

/** Redirects to /login when there is no valid session. */
export const verifySession = cache(async (): Promise<SessionPayload> => {
  const session = await getSession();

  if (!session) {
    redirect("/login");
  }

  // Forced rotation on `users.must_change_password`.
  if (session.mustChangePassword) {
    redirect("/set-password");
  }

  return session;
});

/** Returns the session without redirecting. For optional-auth surfaces. */
export const getOptionalSession = cache(
  async (): Promise<SessionPayload | undefined> => getSession(),
);

/**
 * Session check for the /set-password route itself.
 *
 * `verifySession()` bounces a flagged session to /set-password, so that page
 * cannot call it without looping. This variant requires a valid session but
 * tolerates the `mustChangePassword` flag.
 */
export const verifySessionAllowingPasswordChange = cache(
  async (): Promise<SessionPayload> => {
    const session = await getSession();

    if (!session) {
      redirect("/login");
    }

    return session;
  },
);

/** Client-safe projection. Never leaks `accessToken`. */
export const getCurrentUser = cache(async (): Promise<CurrentUser> => {
  const session = await verifySession();

  return {
    id: session.userId,
    username: session.username,
    displayName: session.username,
    role: session.role,
    employeeId: session.employeeId,
    agentId: session.agentId,
    branchId: session.branchId,
    active: true,
    mustChangePassword: session.mustChangePassword,
  };
});

/** Backend JWT for outbound API calls. Server-side only. */
export const getAccessToken = cache(async (): Promise<string> => {
  const session = await verifySession();
  return session.accessToken;
});

/**
 * Asserts the caller holds `permission`, else bounces to their own home.
 * Use at the top of every Server Action and protected page.
 */
export async function requirePermission(
  permission: Permission,
): Promise<SessionPayload> {
  const session = await verifySession();

  if (!hasPermission(session.role, permission)) {
    redirect(ROLE_HOME[session.role]);
  }

  return session;
}

export async function requireAnyPermission(
  permissions: readonly Permission[],
): Promise<SessionPayload> {
  const session = await verifySession();

  if (!hasAnyPermission(session.role, permissions)) {
    redirect(ROLE_HOME[session.role]);
  }

  return session;
}

/** Asserts the caller's role matches one of `roles`. */
export async function requireRole(
  roles: readonly Role[],
): Promise<SessionPayload> {
  const session = await verifySession();

  if (!roles.includes(session.role)) {
    redirect(ROLE_HOME[session.role]);
  }

  return session;
}

/** Asserts `pathname` is inside the namespace the caller's role owns. */
export async function requireNamespace(
  pathname: string,
): Promise<SessionPayload> {
  const session = await verifySession();

  if (!canAccessPath(session.role, pathname)) {
    redirect(ROLE_HOME[session.role]);
  }

  return session;
}

/**
 * Branch scoping for BRANCH_MANAGER. Roles with company-wide read get `null`
 * (no filter); a branch-scoped role gets its own `branchId`.
 */
export async function getBranchScope(): Promise<number | null> {
  const session = await verifySession();

  if (hasPermission(session.role, "branch:read")) {
    return null;
  }

  return session.branchId;
}


===== src\components\features\auth\agent-login-form.tsx =====
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useActionState } from "react";

import { agentLogin } from "@/app/actions/auth";
import { Icon } from "@/components/ui/icon";
import { PasswordField } from "@/components/shared/password-field";
import { useActionToast } from "@/hooks/use-action-toast";
import type { ActionState } from "@/types";

/**
 * Agent portal sign-in: username + password.
 *
 * Auth note: the backend's login query is `SELECT ... FROM users WHERE
 * username = ?` (see UserRepository / CustomUserDetailsService) â€” there is
 * no separate agent-code credential, only the shared `users.username`
 * column used by every role. The Stitch screen originally asked for an
 * "Agent Code" (KES-XXX-XXX), but posting that as `agentCode` always failed
 * with InvalidCredentialsException since the backend never looks up by
 * agent code. Field renamed to `username` to match what the backend
 * actually authenticates against.
 */
export function AgentLoginForm() {
  const [state, formAction, pending] = useActionState<ActionState, FormData>(
    agentLogin,
    { status: "idle" },
  );

  const error = state.status === "error" ? state : undefined;
  const fieldErrors = error?.fieldErrors;

  // Success redirects; only failures need announcing.
  useActionToast(state);

  return (
    <form action={formAction} className="space-y-5">
      {error && !fieldErrors ? (
        <div
          role="alert"
          className="flex items-start gap-2 rounded-lg border border-error/30 bg-error-container/40 px-3 py-2"
        >
          <Icon name="error" className="mt-px text-[16px] text-error" />
          <p className="font-body-md text-body-md text-on-error-container">
            {error.message}
          </p>
        </div>
      ) : null}

      <div className="relative space-y-1.5">
        <label
          className="block font-label-rail text-label-rail uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface-variant"
          htmlFor="username"
        >
          Username
        </label>
        <div className="relative flex items-center">
          <Icon name="person" className="absolute left-3 text-[18px] text-outline" />
          <input
            id="username"
            name="username"
            type="text"
            required
            autoComplete="username"
            placeholder="Enter your username"
            aria-invalid={fieldErrors?.username ? true : undefined}
            className={`w-full rounded-lg border bg-surface-container-lowest py-2.5 pl-10 pr-4 font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface transition-all placeholder:text-outline-variant focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 ${
              fieldErrors?.username
                ? "border-error focus:border-error focus:ring-error"
                : "border-outline-variant focus:border-primary focus:ring-primary focus:shadow-[0_0_0_2px_rgba(0,85,146,0.1)]"
            }`}
          />
        </div>
        {fieldErrors?.username ? (
          <p className="text-[10px] text-error">{fieldErrors.username[0]}</p>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      <div className="relative space-y-1.5">
        <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
          <label
            className="block font-label-rail text-label-rail uppercase tracking-wider text-on-surface-variant"
            htmlFor="password"
          >
            Password
          </label>
          <Link
            href="/forgot-password"
            className="font-label-rail text-label-rail text-primary transition-colors hover:text-on-primary-fixed-variant"
          >
            Forgot password?
          </Link>
        </div>
        <PasswordField
          required
          leadingIcon="lock"
          invalid={Boolean(fieldErrors?.password)}
          className="py-2.5"
        />
        {fieldErrors?.password ? (
          <p className="text-[10px] text-error">{fieldErrors.password[0]}</p>
        ) : null}
      </div>

      <div className="flex items-center gap-2 pt-2">
        <input
          id="remember"
          name="remember"
          type="checkbox"
          className="h-4 w-4 cursor-pointer rounded border-outline-variant bg-surface-container-lowest text-primary focus:ring-primary"
        />
        <label
          htmlFor="remember"
          className="cursor-pointer select-none font-body-md text-body-md text-on-surface-variant"
        >
          Keep me signed in
        </label>
      </div>

      <button
        type="submit"
        disabled={pending}
        className="group mt-6 flex w-full items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-navy px-4 py-3 font-body-md text-body-md font-bold text-white shadow-md transition-all duration-200 hover:bg-on-primary-fixed-variant hover:shadow-lg disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-70"
      >
        {pending ? (
          <>
            <Icon name="autorenew" className="spin-slow text-[16px]" />
            Signing in...
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            Sign in to Portal
            <Icon
              name="arrow_forward"
              className="text-[16px] transition-transform group-hover:translate-x-1"
            />
          </>
        )}
      </button>
    </form>
  );
}

===== src\components\layout\stub-action-bridge.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { usePathname } from "next/navigation";

import { toast } from "@/lib/toast";

/**
 * Transitional scaffold: makes unwired controls admit that they are unwired.
 *
 * â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
 * WHY THIS EXISTS
 *
 * Most screens in this app still render placeholder data. That left ~270
 * buttons and `href="#"` links that look live, are clicked, and do nothing â€”
 * which users read as "the product is broken", not "this part isn't built".
 *
 * Rather than hand-editing every one of those call sites (and getting some
 * wrong), a single delegated listener reports any click that no real handler
 * claimed.
 *
 * THE CONTRACT â€” read this before adding a working button
 *
 * A control is treated as LIVE, and left alone, when any of these hold:
 *   â€¢ it (or an ancestor) has `data-live`  â† <Button> sets this for you
 *   â€¢ it is a submit/reset button, or sits inside a <form>
 *   â€¢ it is an <a> with a real href (not "#", not empty)
 *   â€¢ it is inside [data-live-region] â€” use this to exempt a whole subtree
 *
 * So: build interactive controls with <Button>/<SubmitButton> from
 * `components/ui/button` and this never fires for them. If you hand-roll a
 * <button onClick={...}>, add `data-live` yourself.
 *
 * DELETE THIS once the screens are wired. It is scaffolding, not architecture â€”
 * its whole job is to be unnecessary.
 * â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
 */
export function StubActionBridge() {
  const pathname = usePathname();

  useEffect(() => {
    function onClick(event: MouseEvent) {
      // Let modified clicks through â€” the user is opening a tab, not acting.
      if (event.defaultPrevented || event.metaKey || event.ctrlKey || event.shiftKey) {
        return;
      }

      const target = event.target as Element | null;
      const control = target?.closest?.("button, a, [role='button']");
      if (!(control instanceof HTMLElement)) return;

      if (isLive(control)) return;

      // An <a href="#"> would otherwise jump the scroll position to the top,
      // which on a long table looks like the page reset itself.
      if (control instanceof HTMLAnchorElement) event.preventDefault();

      toast.notImplemented(labelFor(control));
    }

    document.addEventListener("click", onClick);
    return () => document.removeEventListener("click", onClick);
    // Re-bound per route only so the listener never outlives a navigation in
    // a way that leaks across route-level remounts.
  }, [pathname]);

  return null;
}

function isLive(el: HTMLElement): boolean {
  if (el.closest("[data-live], [data-live-region]")) return true;

  // Anything that submits a form is doing real work â€” logout is the live
  // example today.
  if (el instanceof HTMLButtonElement) {
    if (el.type === "submit" || el.type === "reset") return true;
    if (el.form) return true;
  }

  if (el instanceof HTMLAnchorElement) {
    const href = el.getAttribute("href");
    if (href && href !== "#" && !href.startsWith("#")) return true;
  }

  // Labels drive their own control (file pickers, checkboxes, the search box).
  if (el.closest("label")) return true;

  return false;
}

/** Human label for the toast: visible text, then aria-label, then title. */
function labelFor(el: HTMLElement): string {
  const text = el.textContent?.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim();
  if (text) return truncate(text);

  const aria = el.getAttribute("aria-label") ?? el.getAttribute("title");
  if (aria) return truncate(aria.trim());

  return "This action";
}

function truncate(value: string): string {
  return value.length > 40 ? `${value.slice(0, 39)}â€¦` : value;
}


===== src\config\navigation.ts =====
import type { Role } from "@/config/roles";

/**
 * Rail navigation per role.
 *
 * The Stitch shell is a 96px icon rail, not a 256px sidebar, so there is no
 * room for section headings â€” the tree is flat and the rail scrolls when a
 * role owns more routes than fit. Icons are Material Symbols ligatures
 * (rendered by <Icon />), not Lucide components, because the rail, the topbar
 * and every Stitch screen use the icon font.
 *
 * Every href sits inside the namespace that role owns (ROLE_NAMESPACE), so two
 * roles can never link to the same path.
 *
 * Menu visibility only â€” not an authorization boundary. The DAL re-checks on
 * every page and the Spring AccessGuard is authoritative.
 */

export type NavItem = {
  /** Rail label. Wraps to two lines at 96px, so keep it to two words. */
  title: string;
  href: string;
  /** Material Symbols Outlined ligature, e.g. "dashboard". */
  icon: string;
};

  ],

export function navigationFor(role: Role): readonly NavItem[] {
  return ROLE_NAVIGATION[role];
}

/**
 * True when `pathname` is the item's route or sits beneath it.
 *
 * Matches on a `/` boundary so sibling routes that share a prefix stay
 * distinct â€” `/hr/leave-types` must not light up the `/hr/leave` item.
 */
export function isNavItemActive(pathname: string, href: string): boolean {
  // A route that has its own rail item but lives under another item's prefix
  // must not light up both. `/hr/employees/requests` is "Requests", not "Employees".
  const carvedOut = NESTED_RAIL_ROUTES[href]?.some(
    (route) => pathname === route || pathname.startsWith(`${route}/`),
  );

  if (carvedOut) return false;

  return pathname === href || pathname.startsWith(`${href}/`);
}

/** Parent rail href -> child routes that belong to a different rail item. */
const NESTED_RAIL_ROUTES: Record<string, readonly string[]> = {
  "/hr/employees": ["/hr/employees/requests"],
};

===== src\config\roles.ts =====
/**
 * Role definitions â€” the single source of truth for the frontend.
 *
 * Mirrors `entity/auth/Role.java` exactly. The `users.role` column is a single
 * VARCHAR(30), so a user holds exactly one role.
 *
 * Related: security/AccessGuard, security/CustomUserDetailsService
 */

export const ROLES = [
  "SUPER_ADMIN",
  "ADMIN",
  "HR_MANAGER",
  "PAYROLL_OFFICER",
  "BRANCH_MANAGER",
  "AGENT",
  "EMPLOYEE",
  "FINANCE_OFFICER",
  "AUDITOR",
] as const;

export type Role = (typeof ROLES)[number];

export function isRole(value: unknown): value is Role {
  return typeof value === "string" && (ROLES as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}

/**
 * Which login surface a principal authenticates through.
 *
 * Four surfaces, because the credential differs per population:
 *   STAFF    /login          username + password  (users.username)
 *   EMPLOYEE /employee-login employee no. + PIN   (employees.employee_number)
 *   AGENT    /agent-login    agent code + password
 *   DEVICE   /kiosk          device token         (biometric_devices)
 */
export const PRINCIPAL_TYPES = ["STAFF", "EMPLOYEE", "AGENT", "DEVICE"] as const;
export type PrincipalType = (typeof PRINCIPAL_TYPES)[number];

export const ROLE_PRINCIPAL: Record<Role, PrincipalType> = {
  SUPER_ADMIN: "STAFF",
  ADMIN: "STAFF",
  HR_MANAGER: "STAFF",
  PAYROLL_OFFICER: "STAFF",
  BRANCH_MANAGER: "STAFF",
  AGENT: "AGENT",
  EMPLOYEE: "EMPLOYEE",
  FINANCE_OFFICER: "STAFF",
  AUDITOR: "STAFF",
};

/** Where to send an unauthenticated principal of each type. */
export const PRINCIPAL_LOGIN_ROUTE: Record<PrincipalType, string> = {
  STAFF: "/login",
  EMPLOYEE: "/employee-login",
  AGENT: "/agent-login",
  DEVICE: "/kiosk/clock",
};

/** Login route matching a known role â€” used when re-authenticating. */
export function loginRouteFor(role: Role): string {
  return PRINCIPAL_LOGIN_ROUTE[ROLE_PRINCIPAL[role]];
}

/**
 * URL namespace owned by each role. Every authenticated route sits under
 * exactly one prefix, so two roles can never collide on a path.
 */
export const ROLE_NAMESPACE: Record<Role, string> = {
  SUPER_ADMIN: "/admin",
  ADMIN: "/admin",
  HR_MANAGER: "/hr",
  PAYROLL_OFFICER: "/payroll",
  BRANCH_MANAGER: "/branch",
  AGENT: "/agent",
  EMPLOYEE: "/me",
  FINANCE_OFFICER: "/finance",
  AUDITOR: "/audit",
};

/** Landing route after a successful login. */
export const ROLE_HOME: Record<Role, string> = {
  SUPER_ADMIN: "/admin/dashboard",
  ADMIN: "/admin/dashboard",
  HR_MANAGER: "/hr/dashboard",
  PAYROLL_OFFICER: "/payroll/dashboard",
  BRANCH_MANAGER: "/branch/dashboard",
  AGENT: "/agent/dashboard",
  EMPLOYEE: "/me/dashboard",
  FINANCE_OFFICER: "/finance/dashboard",
  AUDITOR: "/audit/dashboard",
};

export const ROLE_LABEL: Record<Role, string> = {
  SUPER_ADMIN: "Super Admin",
  ADMIN: "Administrator",
  HR_MANAGER: "HR Manager",
  PAYROLL_OFFICER: "Payroll Officer",
  BRANCH_MANAGER: "Branch Manager",
  AGENT: "Agent",
  EMPLOYEE: "Employee",
  FINANCE_OFFICER: "Finance Officer",
  AUDITOR: "Auditor",
};

/**
 * Permissions named `<module>:<action>`, mirroring the backend module layout.
 * Suffixes narrow the data scope:
 *   :self   â€” only the caller's own rows
 *   :branch â€” rows within users.branch_id

 * stays authoritative on every request.

export const PERMISSIONS = [

  "department:read", "department:write",

  "attendance:read", "attendance:read:branch", "attendance:read:self", "attendance:write",

  /** Read-only across the board. Never mutates. */
  AUDITOR: [
    "company:read", "branch:read", "department:read",
    "employee:read", "attendance:read", "shift:read",
    "leave:read", "loan:read", "loan:repayment:read",
    "agent:read", "agentadvance:read",
    "payroll:read", "payslip:read", "agentsummary:read", "invoice:read",
    "report:read", "audit:read",
    "notification:read:self",
  ],
};

export function hasPermission(role: Role, permission: Permission): boolean {
  return ROLE_PERMISSIONS[role].includes(permission);
}

export function hasAnyPermission(
  role: Role,
  permissions: readonly Permission[],
): boolean {
  return permissions.some((p) => hasPermission(role, p));
}

/** True when `pathname` falls inside the namespace this role owns. */
export function canAccessPath(role: Role, pathname: string): boolean {
  const namespace = ROLE_NAMESPACE[role];
  return pathname === namespace || pathname.startsWith(`${namespace}/`);
}


===== src\lib\api\agent.ts =====
import "server-only";

import { api } from "@/app/lib/api-client";
import type { AgentResponse, EmployeeSummaryResponse } from "@/types";

/**
 * `controller/agent/AgentController` â€” base `/agents`.
 *
 * All three reads return bare `ApiResponse<List<â€¦>>`, not a page: the backend
 * loads the whole agent table and there is no `GET /agents` paging. That is
 * fine for a labour-supplier list in the tens; if it ever grows past a few
 * hundred this module is where paging gets added, and the page that calls it
 * will need a `Paginated<T>` in place of the array.
 */

/** Every agent. Open to SUPER_ADMIN, ADMIN and PAYROLL_OFFICER. */
export function getAgents(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<AgentResponse[]> {
  return api.get<AgentResponse[]>("/agents", { signal });
}

/** One agent. Also open to PAYROLL_OFFICER and to the agent itself. */
export function getAgent(
  id: number,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<AgentResponse> {
  return api.get<AgentResponse>(`/agents/${id}`, { signal });
}

/** The signed-in agent's own profile. AGENT principal only. */
export function getMyAgent(signal?: AbortSignal): Promise<AgentResponse> {
  return api.get<AgentResponse>("/agents/me", { signal });
}

/** Staff deployed by one agent. */
export function getAgentStaff(
  agentId: number,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<EmployeeSummaryResponse[]> {
  return api.get<EmployeeSummaryResponse[]>(`/employees/by-agent/${agentId}`, {
    signal,
  });
}

/**
 * Deactivates an agent.
 *
 * SUPER_ADMIN only â€” `AgentController.deactivateAgent` is `hasRole('SUPER_ADMIN')`,
 * while `createAgent`/`updateAgent` accept ADMIN too. The UI must therefore hide
 * this control for ADMIN rather than render it and let the request 403.
 */
export function deactivateAgent(id: number): Promise<void> {
  return api.put<void>(`/agents/${id}/deactivate`);
}


===== src\lib\api\query-keys.ts =====
/**
 * React Query cache keys, one namespace per backend module.
 *
 * Mirrors the `event/` package: when a domain event fires server-side, the
 * matching key below is what the client invalidates. Keeping them in one file
 * makes "what goes stale when payroll completes?" answerable by reading, not
 * grepping.
 */

export const queryKeys = {
  auth: {
    currentUser: ["auth", "current-user"] as const,
  },

  company: {
    all: ["company"] as const,
    detail: (id: number) => ["company", id] as const,
    settings: (id: number) => ["company", id, "settings"] as const,
  },

  branch: {
    all: ["branch"] as const,
    list: (filters?: unknown) => ["branch", "list", filters ?? {}] as const,
    detail: (id: number) => ["branch", id] as const,
  },

  department: {
    all: ["department"] as const,
    list: (filters?: unknown) => ["department", "list", filters ?? {}] as const,
    detail: (id: number) => ["department", id] as const,
  },

  employee: {

      ["attendance", "employee", employeeId, filters ?? {}] as const,

  loan: {

    detail: (id: number) => ["agent", id] as const,
    staff: (id: number) => ["agent", id, "staff"] as const,
  },

  agentAdvance: {
    all: ["agent-advance"] as const,
    list: (filters?: unknown) =>
      ["agent-advance", "list", filters ?? {}] as const,
    detail: (id: number) => ["agent-advance", id] as const,
  },

  payroll: {
    all: ["payroll"] as const,

    run: (id: number) => ["payroll", "run", id] as const,

===== src\lib\api\report.ts =====
import "server-only";

import { fetchDocument } from "@/app/lib/api-client";

/**
 * `controller/report/ReportController` â€” base `/reports`.
 *
 * All three of these return a PDF, not JSON: the controller writes
 * `ResponseEntity<byte[]>` with `Content-Type: application/pdf` and
 * `Content-Disposition: attachment`, deliberately bypassing the
 * `ApiResponse<T>` envelope (wrapping a file's bytes in JSON would corrupt it).
 * So they go through `fetchDocument` rather than `api.get`, and the result is a
 * `Blob` plus the filename the backend chose.
 *
 * Every function here is `hasAnyRole('SUPER_ADMIN', 'ADMIN', 'HR_MANAGER',
 * 'PAYROLL_OFFICER', 'FINANCE_OFFICER', 'AUDITOR')` on the Spring side, and
 * `attendance/{branchId}` additionally admits a BRANCH_MANAGER on their own
 * branch. There is no report an EMPLOYEE or AGENT may generate.
 *
 * These are called from a Route Handler, never from a page: a Server Component
 * cannot hand a `Blob` to the browser, and the bytes must not pass through the
 * RSC payload.
 */

/** Clock activity for one branch over a range. Both bounds are required. */
export function getAttendanceReport(
  branchId: number,
  params: { start: string; end: string },
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<{ blob: Blob; filename: string }> {
  return fetchDocument(`/reports/attendance/${branchId}`, {
    query: { start: params.start, end: params.end },
    signal,
  });
}

/**
 * The register for one completed payroll run.
 *
 * HR may generate this but has no way to *find* a run id: `GET /payroll/runs`
 * does not exist and every `/payroll/runs/{id}` read is restricted to
 * SUPER_ADMIN, ADMIN, PAYROLL_OFFICER, FINANCE_OFFICER and AUDITOR â€” HR_MANAGER
 * is not in that list. The id has to be supplied from outside.
 */
export function getPayrollRegisterReport(
  payrollRunId: number,
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<{ blob: Blob; filename: string }> {
  return fetchDocument(`/reports/payroll-register/${payrollRunId}`, { signal });
}

/**
 * The employee directory as a PDF, for one or more branches.
 *
 * `branchIds` goes on the wire as a repeated query param. Spring binds both
 * that and the comma-separated form the controller's own comment describes to
 * `List<Long>`, and repeating the key is what `buildUrl` does natively.
 */
export function getEmployeeListReport(
  branchIds: number[],
  signal?: AbortSignal,
): Promise<{ blob: Blob; filename: string }> {
  return fetchDocument("/reports/employee-list", {
    query: { branchIds },
    signal,
  });
}


===== src\proxy.ts =====
import { NextResponse, type NextRequest } from "next/server";

import { ROLE_HOME, canAccessPath } from "@/config/roles";

import { decrypt } from "@/app/lib/session";

/**
 * Request proxy. In Next.js 16 this file replaces `middleware.ts` â€”
 * the convention was renamed and the export must be `proxy`.
 *
 * OPTIMISTIC gate only: keeps users out of the wrong namespace and off login
 * screens when already signed in. Trivially bypassable, so every page
 * re-checks through the DAL and every Server Action re-authorizes on its own.
 * Server Functions POST to the route they live on, so a matcher change here
 * can silently drop coverage â€” never rely on this alone.
 */

/** Reachable without a session â€” one per principal type, plus recovery. */
const PUBLIC_PATHS = [
  "/login",
  "/employee-login",
  "/agent-login",
  "/forgot-password",
  "/reset-password",
];

/** Reachable while `must_change_password` is set, and nothing else. */
const PASSWORD_ROTATION_PATH = "/set-password";

function isPublic(pathname: string): boolean {
  return PUBLIC_PATHS.some((p) => pathname === p || pathname.startsWith(`${p}/`));
}

export async function proxy(request: NextRequest) {
  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl;

  // Biometric terminals authenticate by device token, not a user session.
  if (pathname.startsWith("/kiosk")) {
    return NextResponse.next();
  }

  const session = await decrypt(request.cookies.get("dhwarsh_session")?.value);

  if (!session) {
    if (isPublic(pathname)) {
      return NextResponse.next();
    }

    // Staff login is the default door; the footer links cover the others.
    const loginUrl = new URL("/login", request.url);
    if (pathname !== "/") {
      loginUrl.searchParams.set("next", pathname);
    }
    return NextResponse.redirect(loginUrl);
  }

  const home = ROLE_HOME[session.role];

  // Pin a flagged session to the rotation screen until it completes.
  if (session.mustChangePassword) {
    return pathname === PASSWORD_ROTATION_PATH
      ? NextResponse.next()
      : NextResponse.redirect(new URL(PASSWORD_ROTATION_PATH, request.url));
  }

  // Already signed in â€” no reason to sit on a login screen.
  if (isPublic(pathname) || pathname === "/" || pathname === PASSWORD_ROTATION_PATH) {
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL(home, request.url));
  }

  if (pathname === "/legal" || pathname.startsWith("/legal/")) {
    return NextResponse.next();
  }

  // Wrong namespace: an EMPLOYEE poking at /payroll/runs lands back home.
  if (!canAccessPath(session.role, pathname)) {
    return NextResponse.redirect(new URL(home, request.url));
  }

  return NextResponse.next();
}

export const config = {
  // Excludes static assets, image optimization and public files, so auth
  // redirects never swallow CSS, JS or images.
  matcher: [
    "/((?!api|_next/static|_next/image|favicon.ico|.*\\.(?:svg|png|jpg|jpeg|gif|webp|ico)$).*)",
  ],
};


===== src\types\index.ts =====
/**
 * Domain types mirroring `V1__baseline_schema.sql` and the backend
 * `dto/response` package.
 *
 * Conventions:
 *  - `id` is BIGSERIAL server-side â†’ `number` here.
 *  - NUMERIC(15,2) money columns â†’ `number`. Format at the edge, never compute
 *    in the browser; the payroll engine is authoritative.
 *  - DATE â†’ "yyyy-MM-dd", TIMESTAMP â†’ ISO-8601, TIME â†’ "HH:mm:ss".
 */

import type { Role } from "@/config/roles";

export type { Role, Permission, PrincipalType } from "@/config/roles";

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- common -- */

/** BaseEntity columns present on every table. */
export type AuditFields = {
  createdAt: string | null;
  updatedAt: string | null;
  createdBy: string | null;
  updatedBy: string | null;
};

/**
 * Backend `dto/response/PageResponse` â€” a trimmed projection of Spring Data
 * `Page<T>`, not the raw Jackson serialization. There is no `first` flag;
 * derive it from `pageNumber === 0`.
 */
export type Paginated<T> = {
  content: T[];
  pageNumber: number;
  pageSize: number;
  totalElements: number;
  totalPages: number;
  last: boolean;
};

/** Return shape for Server Actions wired to `useActionState`. */
export type ActionState<T = void> =
  | { status: "idle" }
  | { status: "success"; data: T }
  | {
      status: "error";
      message: string;
      fieldErrors?: Record<string, string[] | undefined>;
    };

/* ---------------------------------------------------------- enumerations -- */

/**
 * `branch.status` and `agent.status` â€” a plain `String` column on both entities,
 * defaulting to "ACTIVE".
 *
 * There is no SUSPENDED. Deactivation writes "INACTIVE" and nothing writes
 * anything else (`BranchServiceImpl.deactivateBranch:73`,
 * `AgentServiceImpl.deactivateAgent:68`), so a third member would only ever
 * describe a state the backend cannot produce.
 */
export const STATUSES = ["ACTIVE", "INACTIVE"] as const;
export type Status = (typeof STATUSES)[number];

// Mirrors EmploymentType in the backend entity package:
// PERMANENT, CONTRACT, CASUAL, OUTSOURCED. There is no INTERN.
export const EMPLOYMENT_TYPES = [
  "PERMANENT",
  "CONTRACT",
  "CASUAL",
  "OUTSOURCED",
] as const;
export type EmploymentType = (typeof EMPLOYMENT_TYPES)[number];

// Employee.employmentStatus is a plain String in the backend, defaulted to
// "ACTIVE" and documented there as ACTIVE, INACTIVE, TERMINATED â€” that comment
// is the only definition of the domain, and terminateEmployee() sets
// "TERMINATED". PROBATION / ON_LEAVE / SUSPENDED do not exist anywhere in the
// backend; ON_LEAVE is an *attendance* status, not an employment one.
export const EMPLOYMENT_STATUSES = [
  "ACTIVE",
  "INACTIVE",
  "TERMINATED",
] as const;
export type EmploymentStatus = (typeof EMPLOYMENT_STATUSES)[number];

export const GENDERS = ["MALE", "FEMALE", "OTHER"] as const;
export type Gender = (typeof GENDERS)[number];

export const MARITAL_STATUSES = [
  "SINGLE",
  "MARRIED",
  "DIVORCED",
  "WIDOWED",
] as const;
export type MaritalStatus = (typeof MARITAL_STATUSES)[number];

export const DOCUMENT_TYPES = [
  "CONTRACT",
  "NATIONAL_ID",
  "KRA_PIN",
  "CERTIFICATE",
  "CV",
  "OTHER",
] as const;
export type DocumentType = (typeof DOCUMENT_TYPES)[number];

/**
 * `agents.charge_type` â€” mirrors `entity/agent/ChargeType.java` exactly.
 *
 * The previous list (`FIXED` / `PERCENTAGE`) named values the backend enum does
 * not have, so a form built from it would have POSTed a charge type the server
 * rejects. `AgentRequest.chargeType` is typed as the Java enum, so this is the
 * set that actually round-trips.
 */
export const CHARGE_TYPES = [
  "PER_EMPLOYEE",
  "PERCENTAGE_OF_GROSS",
  "FIXED_MONTHLY",
  "CUSTOM",
] as const;
export type ChargeType = (typeof CHARGE_TYPES)[number];

export const ADVANCE_STATUSES = [
  "PENDING",
  "APPROVED",
  "REJECTED",
  "OUTSTANDING",
  "RECOVERED",
] as const;
export type AdvanceStatus = (typeof ADVANCE_STATUSES)[number];

/** `attendance.source` â€” MANUAL entries are auditable, BIOMETRIC are not editable. */
export const ATTENDANCE_SOURCES = ["BIOMETRIC", "MANUAL", "MOBILE", "WEB"] as const;
export type AttendanceSource = (typeof ATTENDANCE_SOURCES)[number];

export const ATTENDANCE_STATUSES = [
  "PRESENT",
  "ABSENT",
  "LATE",
  "HALF_DAY",
  "ON_LEAVE",
  "HOLIDAY",
] as const;
export type AttendanceStatus = (typeof ATTENDANCE_STATUSES)[number];

export const DEVICE_STATUSES = ["ONLINE", "OFFLINE", "DISABLED"] as const;
export type DeviceStatus = (typeof DEVICE_STATUSES)[number];

/**
 * `entity/shift/ShiftType`.
 *
 * These are shift *types*, not shift names â€” the seeded demo shifts are called
 * "Day", "Night" and "Split", and their types are MORNING, NIGHT and EVENING.
 * A form that offers the names as the type sends values the Java enum rejects.
 */
export const SHIFT_TYPES = [
  "MORNING",
  "EVENING",
  "NIGHT",
  "ROTATING",
  "FLEXIBLE",
] as const;
export type ShiftType = (typeof SHIFT_TYPES)[number];

/**
 * Two-stage approval: `approved_by_manager_id` then `approved_by_hr_id`.
 * PENDING_HR means the branch manager has signed off and HR is next.
 *
 * `entity/leave/LeaveStatus` â€” the values are exactly these four. There is no
 * plain PENDING and no CANCELLED.
 */
export const LEAVE_STATUSES = [
  "PENDING_MANAGER",
  "PENDING_HR",
  "APPROVED",
  "REJECTED",
] as const;
export type LeaveStatus = (typeof LEAVE_STATUSES)[number];

export const LOAN_STATUSES = [
  "PENDING",
  "APPROVED",
  "REJECTED",
  "ACTIVE",
  "SETTLED",
] as const;
export type LoanStatus = (typeof LOAN_STATUSES)[number];

export const PAYROLL_RUN_STATUSES = [
  "DRAFT",
  "PROCESSING",
  "COMPLETED",
  "APPROVED",
  "FAILED",
  "CANCELLED",
] as const;
export type PayrollRunStatus = (typeof PAYROLL_RUN_STATUSES)[number];

export const PAYROLL_PERIODS = ["MONTHLY", "BIWEEKLY", "WEEKLY"] as const;
export type PayrollPeriod = (typeof PAYROLL_PERIODS)[number];

export const NOTIFICATION_CHANNELS = ["IN_APP", "EMAIL", "SMS"] as const;
export type NotificationChannel = (typeof NOTIFICATION_CHANNELS)[number];

/* ------------------------------------------------------------------ auth -- */

/** Projection of `users`, safe to hand to Client Components. */
export type CurrentUser = {
  id: number;
  username: string;
  displayName: string | null;
  role: Role;
  employeeId: number | null;
  agentId: number | null;
  branchId: number | null;
  active: boolean;
  mustChangePassword: boolean;
};

export type UserAccount = CurrentUser & AuditFields;

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- company -- */

export type Company = AuditFields & {
  id: number;
  name: string;
  logoUrl: string | null;
  address: string | null;
  phone: string | null;
  email: string | null;
  website: string | null;
  kraPin: string | null;

  nssfNumber: string | null;

  id: number;
  companyId: number;
  vatRate: number;
  defaultWorkingHoursPerDay: number | null;
